# PROJECT_MAP.md

RDPAudit 2.0 service and configurator solution. Generated from source file headers
(Purpose comments). Paths are relative to the solution root.

## Solution structure

### RdpAudit.Core

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| src/RdpAudit.Core/AbuseIpDb/AbuseIpDbApiKeyValidator.cs | Pure format-validation helper for AbuseIPDB API keys. The public AbuseIPDB v2 keys are // 80-character hexadecimal strings — this validator rejects empty / wrong-length / non-hex // values without ever calling out to the network. It NEVER logs or echoes the key. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/AbuseIpDb/AbuseIpDbCommentBuilder.cs | Pure helper that constructs the AbuseIPDB report "comment" body from RdpAudit evidence. // The comment is professional, sanitised, and intentionally free of local credentials, // command-line content, passwords, tokens, internal hostnames or operator personal data. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/AbuseIpDb/AbuseIpDbReportDecision.cs | Pure decision helpers used by the Stage 8 reporting worker. Keeps dedup, rate-limit and // threshold checks free of EF Core and HTTP so they can be exercised by unit tests. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/AbuseIpDb/AbuseIpDbReportRequest.cs | Plain-data request describing a single AbuseIPDB v2 report submission. The HTTP client // encodes Ip + Categories + Comment into the form-urlencoded body required by the public // /api/v2/report endpoint. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/AbuseIpDb/AbuseIpDbReportResult.cs | Plain-data result describing the outcome of a single AbuseIPDB v2 report submission. // Combines HTTP status, classified outcome and an optional retry-after hint. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/AbuseIpDb/IAbuseIpDbClient.cs | Abstraction over the AbuseIPDB v2 HTTP API used by the Stage 8 reporting worker. The // abstraction lives in Core so unit tests can supply a fake without depending on the // Service host or HttpClient internals. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Backup/BackupCommandBuilder.cs | Composes argument lists for the external command-line tools used by the // Configurator backup/restore feature (auditpol.exe, reg.exe, sc.exe). // Pure list builders — they never spawn processes themselves, never // concatenate user input into a single shell string, and are exercised // by Core unit tests so syntax bugs are caught off-Windows. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Backup/BackupLayout.cs | Pure path math for the backup directory layout used by RdpAudit when // the Configurator captures or restores settings, audit policy, SACL // state and service configuration. No I/O; intentionally Windows-API // free so it is testable on Linux/macOS CI. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Backup/BackupMetadata.cs | JSON metadata document written into every backup snapshot. Documents // when, where, by whom, and which RdpAudit version produced the // snapshot so future restore operations can reason about compatibility. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/ApiOptions.cs | Configuration for the optional read-only HTTPS REST API (roadmap backlog #3). Disabled by default so existing installations expose no new listening surface until an operator opts in; the bearer token is stored as a protected envelope and never logged or echoed over IPC. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/AbuseIpDbOptions.cs | Configuration for the AbuseIPDB external reputation / reporting provider. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/AlertOptions.cs | Defines tunable alert-rule thresholds and switches, including pipeline flood detection. PRIVILEGED_LOGIN hardening controls: EnablePrivilegedLoginDetection, PrivilegedLoginSuppressionWindowMinutes (default 5), PrivilegedLoginRateLimitPerMinute (default 20), and AlertEventMaxAgeMinutes (default 5) - the historical/backfill gate applied by AlertWorker to all rules. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/CrossHostOptions.cs | Configuration for cross-host attack correlation: Enabled switch, agent Hosts list (HostName + protected Credential envelope), poll interval, connect timeout and per-poll fact cap; exposes clamp / resolve helpers consumed by CrossHostCorrelationWorker. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/DecimationOptions.cs | Optional frequency-oriented decimation of raw event telemetry for the retention pass. Disabled by default; every numeric bound resolves through a clamped Resolve* helper so unsafe values fall back to safe limits. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/DiagnosticsOptions.cs | Toggles verbose diagnostic logging while keeping sensitive data out of Information level. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/FirewallBlockScope.cs | Identifies how broadly a firewall block rule restricts traffic from the attacker IP. // RdpPortOnly limits the inbound block to the resolved RDP listener port (least // disruptive, lets other services keep talking to that host); AllInbound blocks every // inbound protocol/port from the remote IP (strongest, default for hostile sources). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/FirewallEnforcementBackend.cs | Selects HOW the local Windows host realises a block beyond the database blocklist row. // WindowsFirewall installs an inbound block rule (fully implemented); RouteBlackhole adds // an experimental per-IP host route to an unreachable next-hop (implemented, but labelled // experimental because Windows has no native Linux-style blackhole route — it mainly // drops outbound replies to the IP); IPsecPolicy is an advanced inbound block filter // surfaced as a clean interface with an explicit status so the UI can disable it cleanly // until the full implementation lands. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/FirewallOptions.cs | Settings for automatic firewall blocking on threat thresholds, provider selection, // static whitelist / blacklist arrays, and instant-block triggers. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/FirewallProviderKind.cs | Identifies which firewall provider(s) RdpAudit should drive when applying block actions. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/IngestionMode.cs | Selects which transport RdpAudit uses to ingest Windows security events from the OS // into the RawEventDto pipeline. v1.x has only EventLogWatcher; v2.x adds a real-time // ETW consumer for lower latency and zero-XML hot path. Auto probes ETW first and // falls back to EventLog when the probe fails (non-Windows host, missing manifest, // insufficient privilege). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/LogsOptions.cs | Operation-log viewing depth and retention settings. Bounds how far back the Logs tab // queries by default and how long durable operation-log rows are kept, so a long-lived // deployment does not let the operation-log table grow without bound or make the Logs tab // slow to open on a large database. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/MikroTikOptions.cs | Configuration for the MikroTik RouterOS v7 external firewall provider (REST API). // Supports either a freeform BaseUrl OR explicit Scheme/Host/Port composition. The // BaseUrl wins when supplied so legacy appsettings.json documents keep binding. Stage 9 // additions: ScheduleAddRules toggle (renamed alias of AddAttackerRules), Scheme/Host/ // Port composition, FilterChain/Action for the firewall filter, a BlockDuration TimeSpan // (composed from days/hours/minutes) and a CommentPrefix used to recognise RdpAudit-owned // rules. The plaintext password must NEVER appear on disk, in logs or in IPC payloads — // it is wrapped by ISecretProtector before SaveSettings persists the document. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/MonitoringConfigRepair.cs | Repairs stale appsettings.json so the Security channel and the v3 authentication // event-ID set are always present in the effective MonitoringOptions, regardless of // what an older config file persisted on disk. Without this fix, upgrades from a pre-v3 // build that pruned EnabledChannels / EnabledEventIds in operator-edited appsettings.json // leave the Security watcher disarmed and the Configurator UI shows Failed=0 even when // PowerShell can see Security 4625 events. // Also clamps an out-of-range IngestionMode enum value to the safe EventLog default so a // hand-edited numeric override cannot poison the ingestion-transport DI selection. // Pure, OS-agnostic, unit-testable. Applied at service startup by Program.Main before // the EventCollectorHostedWorker arms its channels, and surfaced via diagnostics so operators // can confirm the repair fired. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/MonitoringOptions.cs | Configures monitored event sources and bounded pre-pipeline flood protection. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/RdpAuditOptions.cs | Binds root service configuration including optional forensic shard storage. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/RetentionAnchorScope.cs | Identifies the granularity of an information-density retention code: PerCode `(EventId, Channel)`, or PerCodeAndIp which adds the source address so every attacker keeps a first-contact anchor. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/RetentionPolicyOptions.cs | Information-density retention policy: pressure ceilings, anchor scope, protected tail, eligibility threshold, age floor, per-pass and per-writer-lock budgets, delete batch size, legal hold and the protected event classes. Disabled by default; every numeric bound resolves through a clamped Resolve* helper so a hand-edited value cannot widen a limit. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/RingBufferBackend.cs | Selects the concurrency profile of the event pipe ring buffer. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/SessionControlOptions.cs | Configuration for RDP session control actions (disconnect, log off, shadow). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/ShardingOptions.cs | Configures optional durable per-IP forensic shard files. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Config/StorageOptions.cs | Database location, retention windows, log retention settings, and the optional raw-event decimation (Storage.Decimation) and information-density retention (Storage.RetentionPolicy) blocks. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/CrossHost/CrossHostChainAlertFactory.cs | Pure factory that builds CROSS_HOST_CHAIN alert candidates from a correlated chain so the alert worker persists them without touching the correlation engine. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/CrossHost/CrossHostCorrelationEngine.cs | Pure deterministic correlation engine: turns validated fact projections plus per-host availability into deduplicated, bounded attack-chain candidates using data-driven rules (fail-closed on unavailable hosts). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/CrossHost/CrossHostFactModel.cs | Pure in-memory domain model for cross-host correlation: fact projections (AuthAttemptFact summaries), host availability view, correlation result and chain/link candidates; free of EF/SQLite. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/CrossHost/CrossHostRules.cs | Data-driven correlation rule catalogue: ordered hop templates (4625 -> 4624 -> 4672), windows, failed-attempt floor and scoring; ships the default RDP_LATERAL_MOVEMENT rule. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/CrossHost/CrossHostStableHash.cs | SHA-256 based stable hashing for fact dedup keys and chain keys. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/CrossHost/IHostFactSource.cs | Abstraction over a remote agent host's fact pull (consumed by CrossHostCorrelationWorker); implementations must be fail-closed and honour cancellation. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/AuditDbContext.cs | Defines the EF Core database surface for persisted RDPAudit entities. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/AuditDbContextOptions.cs | Single source of truth for the EF Core warning policy applied to every // production / read-only AuditDbContext. RowLimitingOperationWithoutOrderByWarning // is escalated to a compile-at-query-time error: any Skip/Take that runs without a // deterministic prior ORDER BY (or whose order chain is destroyed by a later // Distinct) aborts the query instead of emitting an unstable page that changes // between runs and corrupts pagination. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/AuditDbContextOptionsFactory.cs | Single source of truth for constructing AuditDbContext options. The normal path mirrors Program.RegisterServices (shared-cache connection + the write-enabling SqlitePr... |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/AuditDbInitializer.cs | Applies and serializes database schema initialization before any dependent service component // uses SQLite. On a fatal migration failure it persists a recovery marker under ProgramData so // the Configurator can offer the operator a guided "back up and reset database" flow instead // of leaving the service in a Win32 1067 restart loop with no user-facing recovery affordance. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/Configurations/WhitelistEntryConfiguration.cs | EF Core entity configuration for WhitelistEntry. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/DatabaseMigrationFailureMarker.cs | Persists a machine readable marker describing a fatal EF Core migration failure so the // Configurator can detect the condition on the next launch and offer the operator a // guided recovery (back up the incompatible database, drop it, and let the service create // a fresh one). The marker is written atomically (temp file + File.Move) into ProgramData // so it survives service crashes and SCM restart loops without corrupting itself. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/DbOperationLogWriter.cs | SQLite-backed IOperationLogWriter. Persists each entry on its own short-lived DbContext // (via the context factory) so it is safe to call from any worker / handler scope, and // gates detail fields (stack trace, details JSON) on the global DEBUG-mode setting. Every // write is wrapped so a logging failure self-logs to ILogger and is otherwise swallowed — // the operation-log facility must never be the reason a real action fails or the service // stops. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/DesignTimeAuditDbContextFactory.cs | Allows `dotnet ef` tooling to instantiate AuditDbContext at design time. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/IOperationLogWriter.cs | Abstraction for writing durable operation-log entries (program actions, not security // attack events) from anywhere in the service — IPC handlers, background workers, firewall // and settings code, maintenance and purge paths — without creating a dependency cycle. // Implementations MUST be best-effort: a logging failure must never propagate to or crash // the caller. Detail fields (stack trace, details JSON) are persisted only when DEBUG mode // is enabled so normal operation stays compact. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/Migrations/20260814003237_Stage10IpShardsRetention.cs | Stage 10 adds per-IP aggregate tables (IpEventSummary, IpEventTypeCounter), the durable // IngestionSequence counter, per-event retention and enablement tables, the event-collection // audit trail, and the RawEvents columns that back sharded storage (IngestionSequence, // EventLayer, SourceIpBinary). The IngestionSequence unique index is created as a partial // index (filter IngestionSequence > 0) so pre-existing rows carrying the added-column // default 0 do not violate the constraint on upgrade. Existing rows are backfilled with // ROWID before the index is built, and the IngestionSequence sidecar is seeded past the // highest backfilled value so subsequent ingestion never collides with the backfilled block. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/ReadOnlySqlitePragmaInterceptor.cs | Applies SQLite PRAGMAs that are SAFE on a read-only connection. The regular SqlitePragmaInterceptor issues journal_mode=WAL / synchronous / auto_vacuum, each of which ... |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/SqlitePragmaInterceptor.cs | Applies SQLite PRAGMAs (auto_vacuum, WAL, synchronous, cache, FK, temp_store) on every connection open. auto_vacuum is applied FIRST on purpose: SQLite honours an auto-vacuum change immediately only while a store has no pages, and journal_mode=WAL is what creates page 1, so the order is what makes a store this product creates start in INCREMENTAL mode and lets the online reclamation steps release anything at all (B27). On an existing store the statement can only become pending, so the order changes nothing observable there. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Data/SqliteTransientErrors.cs | Single classifier for the SQLite failures the workers may retry: walks the exception chain so a busy/locked code is still recognised when EF Core has wrapped it in DbUpdateException, and deliberately refuses to retry deterministic failures such as a constraint violation. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/BenchmarkMeasurement.cs | One measured - or explicitly not measured, or failed - quantity in a storage benchmark run. "Not measured" is a first-class state rather than a zero, so a metric the run never reached reads as absent with a reason and never as a plausible-looking value. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/BenchmarkSampleSet.cs | The sample statistics every storage benchmark metric is reported with: median (the value a report quotes) plus p95 by nearest rank, mean, min and max. Pure and deterministic, so the arithmetic that turns samples into a published number is unit-testable without a machine. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/MachineProfile.cs | The machine a storage benchmark ran on, as data: operating system and build, .NET runtime, processor and core count, physical memory, elevation, and the store volume (type, capacity, free space, system-volume flag, solid-state evidence with its source). Facts that could not be collected are reported as unknown and named, never guessed. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/OverviewProgressState.cs | Thread-safe, in-memory holder for the live state of the long-running historical // analysis / backfill / indexing job. A background worker publishes progress here as it // works through a large historical backlog; the GetOverviewProgress IPC handler reads a // consistent snapshot so the Configurator's Overview tab can render a progress bar without // blocking on a full historical scan. Kept in Core (not Service) so both the worker and the // IPC handler can depend on it without a project cycle. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/StorageBenchmarkMetric.cs | The stable vocabulary of the storage benchmark report: one identifier per measurable quantity, so the probe that measures it, the recommendation engine that consumes it and the report writer that prints it cannot disagree about its name. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/StorageBenchmarkUnit.cs | The units a storage benchmark metric can be quoted in (rows/s, ms, bytes, MiB/s, ...). A number without its unit is not a measurement, so the unit travels with the metric into the report's value column, its JSON and every recommendation rationale. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/StorageBenchmarkReport.cs | The complete, versioned result of one storage benchmark run: machine profile, methodology, measurements, recommendations and limitations. It is the single object both report writers render and the object the JSON round-trip test deserializes, so the two reports cannot disagree about a number. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/StorageBenchmarkReportWriter.cs | Renders a storage benchmark report three ways: human-readable Markdown, a versioned machine-readable JSON document, and a settings fragment an operator may merge by hand. Pure string builders plus a thin file-writing shell, so the exact text of a report is testable without touching a disk. It cannot apply a setting and no caller of it can either. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/StorageTuningAdvisor.cs | Pure recommendation engine: machine profile plus measured quantities in, parameter advice out. No I/O, no clock, deterministic. A rule whose input is missing proposes NOTHING and names the missing metric, because a plausible default wearing the label "measurement" is the failure this feature exists to prevent. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/StorageTuningTargets.cs | The operational goals and safety factors the recommendation rules are written in terms of (target pass duration, writer-lock headroom factor, free-space headroom, integrity-check schedule thresholds, rounding steps). Kept as data so a recommendation is reviewable and a test can exercise a rule at a different goal. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Diagnostics/TuningRecommendation.cs | One parameter the recommendation engine has an opinion about, with the evidence for it: the rule, the rule with this run's numbers substituted in, the measured inputs consumed, the caveat that bounds it, and whether it is a real settings key or operational advice. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Editions/EditionContext.cs | Resolves which edition this build was compiled for (RDPAUDIT_EDITION_* constants) and answers capability questions about it. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Editions/EditionFeature.cs | The capabilities that are not part of the Community edition, one member per capability that has a real enforcement seam in the code. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Editions/EditionMatrix.cs | Single source of truth for the open-core matrix: minimum edition per capability, cross-host host budget per edition, display and licence names, denial messages. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Editions/ProductEdition.cs | The commercial editions of the product, ordered by capability; the ordinal is part of the IPC surface and is append-only. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/AlertRuleBase.cs | Convenience base class for IAlertRule implementations. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/AuditPolicyManager.cs | Provides the canonical list of auditpol subcategories required by RdpAudit and // executes auditpol.exe / SACL configuration when running on Windows with elevation. // Uses GUID subcategory identifiers for non-English Windows compatibility. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/AuthSuccessExportFormatter.cs | Pure file-export formatter for the RDP Activity "Export Auth Success (per login)" action. // Renders an AuthSuccessSummaryDto — the per-login (NormalizedUserName) roll-up of // AuthAttemptFacts for one attacker IP — into JSON / TXT / Markdown / CSV. The report is // deliberately summary only: one row per account, never one row per attempt. Each login row // answers the incident-analyst questions "was this login's password guessed from this IP?", // "how many failed attempts did it take?", "how long did it take?", "which events/logon // types/auth packages proved the success?" and "which failure reasons were seen?". Embedded // tabs / CR / LF / pipes are neutralised so a pasted row never breaks the structure. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/BookmarkRecordIdParser.cs | Zero-allocation parser extracting the RecordId decimal from the Win8+ EventLogWatcher // bookmark XML (<BookmarkList><Bookmark Channel='...' RecordId='N' IsCurrent='true'/>), so // startup diagnostics can log the exact resumed position (D1 acceptance criterion). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/BookmarkStore.cs | Thread-safe persisted store of EventLogWatcher bookmark XML strings keyed by channel. // v2.0 adds raw-SQLite UPSERT that shares an external SqliteTransaction with the event // batch, so bookmarks and their events cross a single durability boundary. The legacy EF // API (SaveBookmarkAsync, DeleteBookmarkAsync, LoadAllAsync, GetBookmarkXml) is preserved // bit-for-bit so existing callers keep working during the migration. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/ConnectionFactsExportFormatter.cs | Pure clipboard / file-export formatters for the Stage IP-E "Export Connection Facts" // action. All non-CSV formats include the per-IP summary header // (IP, first/last seen, failed/successful totals, active status, fact count, generated // time). CSV stays a clean tabular fact stream — no prose preamble — so the file loads // directly into Excel or downstream tools. Embedded tabs / CR / LF / pipes are // neutralised so a pasted row never breaks the structure. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EtwProviderMap.cs | Static map from a Windows event-log channel name (as declared in EventCatalog) to the // ETW provider that supplies its events in real time, along with a boolean flag that // says whether an in-process TraceEventSession can subscribe to that provider without // administrative work-arounds. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventChannelHealth.cs | D7 - pure three-state health classification for an event-log channel prerequisite: // Ok / Disabled / Missing. The Configurator's PrerequisiteChecker folds EventLogConfiguration // observations through this so a disabled channel (fixable via wevtutil) is told apart // from a channel absent on this Windows build/SKU (no fix offered). Dependency-free and // unit-testable off-Windows. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventCatalog.cs | Static catalog of every Windows event id monitored by RdpAudit. Each entry carries // channel, layer, preset memberships, criticality, correlation dependencies, and a // default retention window that the Configurator and RetentionWorker consult. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventCriticality.cs | Ordered severity classification for a monitored Windows event id. Drives Configurator // colour-coding, default alert routing, and forensic retention floors. Values are // numerically ordered so callers can compare with <, > without a lookup table. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventDescriptor.cs | Static metadata about a single Windows event id we monitor. Positional signature is // kept intact so every existing `new(id, channel, description, layer)` call in the // codebase continues to compile; new fields are init-only properties with defaults. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventFloodGuard.cs | Bounds per-channel and per-source event flood accounting without allocating on the ingestion path. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventLayer.cs | Typed layer classification for a monitored Windows event id. Complements the string // Layer field on EventDescriptor (kept for UI-backward-compatibility) with a byte- // sized enum suitable for persistence in RawEvents.EventLayer and shard records. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventPreset.cs | [Flags] set of preset memberships for a monitored event id. A single event may // participate in several presets (e.g. 4625 is in Minimal, Essential, and Full). // Each preset is one bit, so preset intersection and union are single AND/OR ops. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/EventXmlParser.cs | Hardened, back-compatible XML parser for Windows EventRecord serialized payloads. // Public surface (ParseSafe / GetData / GetInt / GetDataAt) is preserved 1:1 so // every existing caller (EventNormalizer, PerEventIpResolver, SecurityAuthProbeService, // existing tests) keeps working. Internal handling is hardened against XXE, entity // expansion, oversized documents, DTD injection, IO faults and malformed encoding. // Adds a new 2.0 helper set (ExtractSourceIp, ExtractLogonId, ExtractSessionId, // ExtractActivityId, PopulateFrom) that fills the new RawEventDto correlation fields // from event payloads. All heap allocations stay confined to the (documented) XmlDocument // construction — DOM is required by the legacy contract; the new helpers avoid it. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/IAlertContext.cs | Lookup primitives provided to alert rules (recent events, address reputation, options). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/IAlertRule.cs | Contract every alert detection rule must implement. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/IEventPipe.cs | Zero-allocation, single-writer-or-multi-writer conduit that decouples the producer // (IEventSource / EventCollectorWorker) from the consumer (EventProcessorWorker). // Abstracting the pipe behind an interface lets v2.0 swap the underlying transport // — today an SPSC ring buffer, tomorrow an MPMC ring buffer with cache-line padding, // without touching a single Worker. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/IEventSource.cs | Transport-agnostic contract for whatever "produces" RawEventDto instances into an // IEventPipe. v1.x lives on EventLogWatcher (EventLogWatcherEventSource). v2.x will // add ETW real-time consumers, direct EVTX binary readers, and cross-boundary IPC // shims — all behind this one interface, so the rest of the service does not care. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/IpEventsExportFormatter.cs | Pure clipboard / file-export formatters for the Stage A "Export All IP Events" action. // Renders an EventsForIpDto plus its bounded RawEvents window into JSON / TXT / Markdown / // CSV. All non-CSV formats include the summary header (IP, attack type, first/last UTC, // failed/success counts, attempted usernames, duration, threat level, block status); CSV // stays a clean tabular event stream so it loads cleanly into Excel or downstream tools. // Embedded tabs / CR / LF are neutralised for TXT / CSV so a pasted row never breaks the // structure. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/LiveEventFilter.cs | Pure, UI-agnostic filter specification + predicate for the LiveEvents grid. Stage 4 // extracts this into Core so it can be unit-tested independently of WinForms and the // Configurator project. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/LiveEventRowFormatter.cs | Pure formatters for the LiveEvents "Copy Event Details" context-menu action. Lives in // Core so the multiline and TSV serialisations are unit-testable without WinForms. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/LogonTypeCatalog.cs | Pure, UI-free catalog that decodes a Windows Security "Logon Type" numeric code // (as carried in Security 4624 / 4625 events) into an operator-friendly name and a // one-line human-readable description. Used by the Configurator to render the // "Last LogonType" cell hover tooltip and by exporters that annotate raw codes. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/NtStatusFormatter.cs | Canonicalises NTSTATUS values that surface in Security 4625/4776/4771 EventData fields. // Windows writes the same NTSTATUS in at least three different textual forms depending on // the producer and version: hex with the "0x" prefix (e.g. "0xC000006A"), unsigned // decimal (e.g. "3221225578"), and signed decimal int32 (e.g. "-1073741715") — and on // freshly-installed hosts the *signed* form is by far the most common because PowerShell // and many built-in views render Properties[Status] as Int32. Without a single canonical // string the SubStatus dictionary lookup misses, the SQL/EF column varies between rows, // and attack-statistics counters never line up. This helper produces one canonical // "0xXXXXXXXX" form per code, accepts every textual variant we have observed in the wild, // and is safe to call on user-controlled values (no exceptions on garbage input). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/PerEventIpResolver.cs | Per-(channel,eventId) IP extraction from EventRecord XML. Returns only validated // IP addresses; hostname-only fields (ClientName, Workstation, WorkstationName) are // never consulted, so a NetBIOS name can never leak into RawEvent.SourceIp. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/RawEventDto.cs | In-memory DTO carrying a captured EventRecord through the ingestion pipeline. Extended // in 2.0 with correlation ids (ActivityId / LogonId / SessionId), an IngestionSequence for // exactly-once accounting, a typed EventLayer, and a 16-byte SourceIpBinary for the IPv4- // in-IPv6 sharding path. Every new field carries a safe default so all existing // `new RawEventDto { ... }` object-initialiser calls continue to compile unchanged. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/RdpServiceAccountCatalog.cs | Pure, UI-free catalog that decodes the well-known Windows service / built-in / RDP // session account names (DWM-1, UMFD-0, SYSTEM, LOCAL SERVICE, "$"-suffixed machine // accounts, …) into a human-readable explanation. The "Top 10 Attempted Logins" column // frequently mixes genuine attacker usernames with these internal accounts, so the // Configurator uses this catalog to append a legend to that column's header tooltip. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/SaclManager.cs | Configures registry SACLs for IFEO, Terminal Server RDP-Tcp, and Lsa keys so that // object-access audit events fire for the rules STICKY_KEYS_BACKDOOR, RDP_PORT_CHANGED, // LSASS_PPL_TAMPER. Uses Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey.GetAccessControl APIs and // SeSecurityPrivilege via the running elevated process. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/SecurityAuthQuery.cs | Canonical narrow XPath builder for Security-channel authentication ingestion. // The push-based EventLogWatcher cannot honour ReverseDirection / MaxEvents — on a // host with a multi-gigabyte Security log a broad catalog scan stalls forever and // reports Armed-but-zero-events. cameyo/rdpmon and PowerShell both succeed because // they pin the query to a tiny canonical auth-event set; mirror that contract here // so the realtime watcher and the bounded backfill / probe paths all share one // XPath shape. 4624 / 4625 / 4648 / 4768 / 4769 / 4771 / 4776 / 4825 are the v3 // atomic-outcome carriers (Detect_Attack_Strategy_v3.md §6.3, §8.1). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/SubStatusCatalog.cs | Translation table for Windows Security event 4625 / 4776 / 4771 SubStatus codes per // Detect_Attack_Strategy_v3.md §3.1 "SubStatus Code Reference". Exposes a single // dictionary-style lookup so UI rendering, log formatting, and tests share one canonical // string per code. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Events/SuccessfulSessionsExportFormatter.cs | Pure clipboard / file-export formatter for the "Export Successful RDP Sessions" action. // Takes the same ConnectionFactsForIpDto used by Export Connection Facts, keeps only the // facts that represent a genuinely successful RDP session (SuccessfulLogons > 0 OR a // Connected/Authenticated lifecycle timestamp is present), and renders — per session — // the exact evidence the "session is successful" decision was based on: the decision // event ids decoded to their meaning, and the lifecycle timestamps. Emits JSON / TXT / // Markdown / CSV. Embedded tabs / CR / LF / pipes are neutralised so a pasted row never // breaks the structure. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/BackendCommandAttempt.cs | Captures the full backend-command detail of a single firewall block / verify attempt so // per-IP diagnostics can show exactly what ran instead of an opaque "Failed / Failed". Carries // the resolved executable, argument line, runner mode, exit code, timeout flag, wall-clock // duration, and bounded stdout / stderr previews. When the backend exits non-zero with empty // stderr the stdout preview is the only failure signal, so it is always populated independently. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/BlockExpiryCalculator.cs | v1.3.9 — single source of truth for the ExpiresUtc of a newly created or updated block. // The Firewall tab showed "Never" for manual blacklist / Active block rows even when the // operator had configured a positive DefaultBlockDurationMinutes, because the manual-add IPC // path ignored that option and only honoured an explicit per-request duration. This pure // calculator centralises the precedence so the manual-add path, the auto-block worker and the // unit tests all agree: an explicit positive duration wins; otherwise the configured default // applies; a duration that resolves to exactly zero (no explicit, no default) means a // permanent block (ExpiresUtc == null → rendered "Never"). Pure / no I/O — fully testable. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/BlockExpiryFormatter.cs | Pure, culture-invariant formatters for an ActiveBlock's expiry column and its remaining // time, shared by the Configurator's Active Blocks grid and unit tests. A permanent // (manual, never-expiring) block renders its expiry as "Never" and its remaining time as // "Permanent"; a still-active timed block renders a compact "1d 02h 03m" remaining; an // already-past expiry renders "Expired". Keeping this here (not in WinForms) makes the // "manual permanent = Never, auto = remaining" contract verifiable without a UI host. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/EnforcementReconciliation.cs | Pure domain model + engine for live enforcement reconciliation. RdpAudit must never // claim an IP is actively blocked unless a real backend object exists and matches the // expected parameters. This file separates the four states the system actually has — // DB intent (blocklist), recorded enforcement (ActiveBlock rows), discovered backend // objects (live firewall / route / IPsec scan), and the reconciled effective confidence — // and the reconciler that maps them to a single EnforcementStatus + EnforcementConfidence // per (provider, ip). The engine is Win32-free and EF-free so it is unit-testable cross // platform; the Service layer feeds it pre-read facts (desired blocks + discovered rules). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallActionResult.cs | Result DTO returned by IFirewallProvider Block / Unblock / List operations. Carries a // stable status discriminator plus an operator-facing message free of secret data. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallBlockEntry.cs | DTO returned by IFirewallProvider.ListBlocksAsync describing one active block rule. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallBlockRequest.cs | Safe request DTO carried into IFirewallProvider.BlockAsync. Captures the source IP, // base rule name, optional duration, and operator-supplied reason text. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallDiagnosticsReport.cs | Pure formatter for the "Copy firewall diagnostics" report. Aggregates the firewall- // enforcement facts an operator needs to confirm that RdpAudit's IP blocking is actually // in effect: configured provider/backend, the resolved (non-hardcoded) RDP listener port, // per-provider availability, RdpAudit-group inbound block rules present in the Windows // firewall store, enabled-allow inbound TCP ports (so a stale 3389 allow rule is visible // next to the real listener port), route-blackhole / IPsec backend state, third-party // firewall (e.g. Kaspersky) interference notes, and a reconciliation of active enforcement // against blocklist / active-block database rows. Produces a single English block the // operator can paste into a support ticket. Pure formatting; no I/O. Callers pass pre-read // facts so this stays unit-testable cross-platform and never depends on Win32 / EF. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallProviderClassifier.cs | Pure classifier that derives a FirewallProviderDetectedKind + provider name from a // captured snapshot of services / CLI tools / Windows Firewall profiles. Kept Win32-free // so the same rules can be unit-tested without spawning a real Windows host. Every // collection walk below uses an indexed for-loop rather than foreach: iterating an // IReadOnlyList<T>-typed parameter via foreach forces the C# compiler to dispatch through // IEnumerable<T>.GetEnumerator(), which boxes List<T>'s struct enumerator on the heap — // a per-call allocation that violates the zero-alloc hot-path directive even though no // `new`, string interpolation, or LINQ appears anywhere in this file. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallProviderDiagnostics.cs | Pure detection / diagnostics models for the firewall environment surrounding the // RDP listener: which Windows Firewall profiles are enabled, which third-party // security products (Kaspersky in particular) appear to be present, and whether // direct Windows Firewall rule management is expected to succeed. The detection // providers live in the platform-specific hosts (Configurator touches Win32 // services/processes/WMI/filesystem; Service touches ServiceController); the pure // result models and diagnostic-text formatter live here so both hosts and unit // tests can consume the same shape without any Win32 dependency. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallProviderRouting.cs | Pure mapping from the configured provider kind + local enforcement backend to the // stable IFirewallProvider.ProviderId string. Centralises the routing so the auto-block // and expiration workers agree on which provider services a given block, and so the // mapping is unit-testable without DI. The "local" host provider (FirewallProviderKind // Windows) is dispatched to WindowsFirewall / RouteBlackhole / IPsec by the backend; // MikroTik is always the external RouterOS provider regardless of backend. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallProviderStatus.cs | Discriminator for the operational state of a single firewall provider implementation. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/FirewallStatusReport.cs | Snapshot of a firewall provider's current readiness, returned by IFirewallProvider.GetStatusAsync. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/IFirewallProvider.cs | Abstraction for any firewall provider RdpAudit can drive (Windows Advanced Firewall, // MikroTik RouterOS, future cloud providers). All methods are async and honour the // supplied CancellationToken; never block on .Result / .Wait. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/IThirdPartyFirewallProbe.cs | Platform-agnostic contract for collecting raw service/CLI facts that // FirewallProviderClassifier needs to determine whether a third-party // firewall (Kaspersky, ESET, Bitdefender, etc.) is present. Separates // the Win32 data-collection concern from the pure classification logic // so both Service and Configurator share a single classifier while each // supplies facts through its own probe implementation. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/LocalRulePolicyParser.cs | Pure parser for `netsh advfirewall show allprofiles` output that surfaces the // `LocalFirewallRules` (and related `LocalConSecRules`) policy hints. On hosts where // group policy forces firewall rules to live in the GPO store, netsh emits // `LocalFirewallRules N/A (GPO-store only)` instead of `Enable`/`Disable`. Stage 4 // diagnostics need to recognise this row so the "Windows Firewall RDP rule present" // probe can explain to the operator that local writes are not allowed by policy — // rather than reporting a generic netsh failure. // // The parser is intentionally lenient: it accepts case variations and trailing notes // such as "(GPO-store only)" and reports a single tri-state per profile when the row // is present. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/NetshDiagnosticsFormatter.cs | Pure formatter that converts a captured netsh probe outcome (command, args, exit // code, stdout, stderr, configured port) into a single actionable diagnostic string. // Replaces the previous "netsh exit 1" detail with explicit operator-facing context // including the rule name attempted, the configured RDP port, the captured streams, // and — when supplied — the firewall provider environment. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/NetshRuleScanner.cs | Pure parser that scans the textual output of `netsh advfirewall firewall show rule // name=all verbose` for an inbound-allow rule whose LocalPort matches a configured RDP // port. The parser relies on the stable English keys netsh emits regardless of the // operator's UI culture (when the producer pinned the console to chcp 437): // `Rule Name`, `Enabled`, `Direction`, `Action`, `Protocol`, `LocalPort`. // // Stage 4 hardening: // * `Enabled: Yes` is now required — disabled rules no longer satisfy the probe. // * `Protocol: TCP` is now required — UDP / Any rules no longer satisfy the probe. // * A diagnostic helper enumerates every rule block that matches the requested port // but failed one of the other gates, so the operator sees "you have a rule for // 3389 while the listener is on 55554" or "the rule is disabled" instead of a // generic 'no match'. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/PowerShellFirewallRuleParser.cs | Pure parser for the JSON produced by `Get-NetFirewallRule | … | ConvertTo-Json`. Unlike // the verbose `netsh` text dump (whose field labels are translated into the operator's UI // language — "Имя правила:" on a Russian host instead of "Rule Name:"), the JSON property // names emitted by ConvertTo-Json are English-stable on every locale. This parser is the // locale-independent primitive behind PowerShellFirewallRuleScanner; the netsh text scanner // is retained only as a clearly-labeled fallback. Pure: no I/O, no Win32, fully testable // cross-platform from canned JSON. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/RdpAuditFirewallRuleMatcher.cs | v1.3.8 — recognise RdpAudit block rules for a given IP across BOTH identity forms that // Windows Firewall can present, and report duplicate / canonicalization status so the // Firewall tab can reconcile desired vs. actual enforcement honestly. On the affected host // a single blocked IP surfaced as TWO firewall rules: // 1. canonical — Name == "RdpAudit-Block-<ip>", Group == "RdpAudit"; // 2. GUID-named — Name == "{GUID}", DisplayName == "RdpAudit-Block-<ip>", Group empty. // The prior verifier matched only on Name-prefix OR Group, so the GUID-named rule (Name is // a GUID, Group empty) was MISSED — enforcement showed "MISSING RULE" even though a valid // block rule existed, and a manual "Repair Selected" was needed. This pure matcher closes // that gap: it matches the canonical Name, the DisplayName form, and the Group, dedupes by // IP, flags duplicates, and recommends the canonical rule for repair. Pure / no I/O — fully // testable from canned rule tuples cross-platform. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Firewall/RouteBlackholeCommandBuilder.cs | Pure builders + validators for the experimental route-blackhole enforcement backend. // Windows has no native Linux-style discard/blackhole route, so this backend installs a // per-IP host route ("route add <ip> mask 255.255.255.255 <unreachable-gateway>") that // points the attacker IP at an unreachable next-hop, dropping outbound replies. Every // helper validates the IP and gateway defensively and emits arguments that can ONLY be // passed through ProcessStartInfo.ArgumentList — no shell concatenation, ever. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Interop/NativeMethods.cs | Source-generated P/Invoke declarations only — no logic. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/AuthSuccessSummaryDto.cs | Response payload for GetAuthSuccessSummaryForIp. It never carries // one row per attempt: each entry is a single rolled-up account so the report stays compact // even for IPs with tens of thousands of attempts. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/AuthSuccessSummaryRequest.cs | Bounded request shape for GetAuthSuccessSummaryForIp. Carries the target IP, a login-row // cap the server clamps, and a flag choosing between "successful logins only" (the default, // keeps the report compact) and "every observed login". |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/ConnectionFactDto.cs | DTO projection of one RdpConnectionFact row delivered over IPC. MessagePack keys are append-only — never reuse a deleted ordinal. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/ConnectionFactsDto.cs | Response shapes for ListConnectionFacts (recent + filtered list) and // GetConnectionFactsForIp (per-IP detail summary). Both responses carry a bounded fact // collection plus aggregate counters useful for the Configurator's Attack Statistics // augmentation and the per-IP export header. MessagePack keys are append-only. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/ConnectionFactsRequest.cs | Bounded request shape for ListConnectionFacts (recent + filtered) and GetConnectionFactsForIp // (per-IP detail). Carries optional IP/User filters, an optional time window, and a // requested row limit that the server clamps. MessagePack keys are append-only. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/CrossHostChainDetailsDto.cs | Response payload for GetCrossHostChainDetails: full ordered-hop breakdown (CrossHostChainLinkDto[]) of one chain plus ack state; MessagePack keys append-only. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/CrossHostChainDto.cs | Response shapes for ListCrossHostChains: bounded, newest-first chain list rows carrying ChainKey, rule, score, failed-attempt count, ordered HostPath and ack state; MessagePack keys append-only. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/OperationLogQueryRequest.cs | Request DTO for QueryOperationLogs: bounded, filtered, paged query over the durable // operation-log table for the Configurator's Logs tab. Append-only [Key] // indices preserve cross-version IPC compatibility. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/RdpConfigurationDto.cs | DTO describing the current RDP listener configuration that the new RDP Configuration // tab renders — port, enabled flag, NLA / SecurityLayer, single-session, hide-users // policy values, session shadowing mode, plus service / version context. | src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/RdpSessionDto.cs | DTO describing a live RDP session for the ListRdpSessions IPC command. |

These let the Configurator // build the Active Blocks view from reconciliation results — never DB rows alone — and // surface verify / repair / cleanup outcomes to the operator. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/SecurityAuthProbeDto.cs | Outcome envelope returned by the RunSecurityAuthProbe IPC command. Distinguishes the // three failure modes operators conflate when the Security channel appears "stuck": // access denied (service account lacks the Manage auditing and security log right), // query timeout (massive Security log + slow disk), and zero events (audit policy off // or no recent auth). The probe is a one-shot 24h ReverseDirection=true read of up to // 20 Security 4624/4625 events that runs inside the service process so it observes // exactly what the long-running collector sees. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/SessionActionResult.cs | DTO returned by session-control IPC commands with a stable status discriminator and // a human-readable operator-facing message. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/ShadowPolicyStatusDto.cs | DTO describing the current Terminal Services shadow policy plus backup metadata. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/Contracts/ToolsDiagnosticsDto.cs | IPC DTOs for the Tools Diag tab. ToolProbeResultDto captures one external-command probe with // full runner metadata (tool name, resolved executable, arguments, runner mode, working dir, // exit code, duration, timed-out flag, stdout / stderr previews, detected locale hint and a // pass/fail verdict). ToolsDiagnosticsDto bundles the probe set plus a single-block report text // the operator can copy / save. The temporary-firewall-rule probe reuses ToolProbeResultDto for // each of its create / verify / cleanup steps and reports the rule name, handle and backend. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/IpcCommand.cs | Defines the append-only IPC command ABI shared by the Configurator and Service. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/IpcConstants.cs | Shared constants for the Named Pipe IPC channel, including per-command operation // timeouts. Long-running maintenance commands (firewall Repair / Verify / Tools Diag / // temporary-rule probe) spawn several netsh / PowerShell processes and routinely exceed // the default short timeout; capping those at 5 s caused the client to abandon a request // the service was still completing, which the UI then misread as "service not running". |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/IpcException.cs | Exception type thrown by IPC client / server protocol failures. |

| src/RdpAudit.Core/Ipc/ServiceStatus.cs | Snapshot of runtime service health, surfaced via the IPC GetStatus command. |

Implementations MUST never log plaintext credentials, // never delete rules they did not author (comment-prefix check) and surface controlled // MikroTikOperationResult values for every code path. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/MikroTikOperationResult.cs | Classified result returned by IMikroTikClient operations. | src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/MikroTikRule.cs | Plain-data DTOs describing a MikroTik RouterOS v7 firewall filter rule from the // service's perspective: the request supplied to AddBlockAsync, and the projected row // returned by ListBlocksAsync. Neither shape carries credentials. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/MikroTikSetupCommands.cs | Pure helper that exposes the RouterOS v7 shell command bundle used by the // Configurator MikroTik tab. The bundle is split into named sections so unit tests can // assert that required placeholders are present and that no plaintext secret values are // ever embedded. Operators substitute the placeholders before pasting into Winbox / // terminal — the helper itself only emits placeholder tokens. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/MikroTikUrlBuilder.cs | Pure helper that composes the base REST URL for a MikroTik RouterOS v7 endpoint from a // MikroTikOptions instance. Validates host syntax and rejects schemes / hosts that could // turn the outbound HTTP call into a request to an unexpected target. Tested without any // HTTP dependency in RdpAudit.Core.Tests. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/SecurityAudit/SecurityAuditTypes.cs | Value types of the RouterOS security audit: severity and autofix classification, the operator // profile, one catalogue rule, one finding, and the finished report with the fixed risk weights // (critical 40, high 15, medium 5, low 1, info 0, capped at 100). Plain data only — no HTTP, no clock. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/SecurityAudit/MikroTikSecuritySnapshot.cs | Read-only RouterOS facts one audit run is evaluated against: rows per menu, unreadable menus with their // error, menus the device does not have, and the pasport fields. Truthy() accepts both REST true/false // and console yes/no — the difference that once hid every open service on RouterOS 7.23.1. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/SecurityAudit/SecurityAuditSections.cs | The exact menus the audit reads — section key, REST path, singleton-or-array shape, console notation. // The single place that decides what one audit run covers, so a new rule cannot silently read nothing. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/SecurityAudit/SecurityAuditCatalog.cs | The rule catalogue (S-00 … S-35 plus S-27b) as data: id, severity, menu, why it matters, what to do, // autofix class (auto / manual / risky / none). Ported from the validated Python reference; the text is // English because every operator-facing string in this product is. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/SecurityAudit/SecurityAuditEvaluator.cs | The checks themselves: pure functions over a snapshot producing findings, risk index and verdict // without touching HTTP, the clock or the file system, so every rule is regression-tested by xunit on a // fabricated device. An absent menu stays silent; an unreadable one becomes S-00. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/SecurityAudit/SecurityAuditMarkdown.cs | Renders a report as Markdown: summary with the risk index and verdict, findings table, per-finding // reason, recommendation and ready RouterOS commands, and the explicit list of menus not evaluated. // Formatting only — the evaluator has already redacted every secret. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/MikrotikConfig.cs | Source module (no Purpose header). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/MikroTik/MikrotikIpcMessages.cs | Source module (no Purpose header). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AbuseIpDbReportHistory.cs | Persistent, audit-grade history of every AbuseIPDB report ATTEMPT (success or failure). // Drives the success-filtered report cooldown / dedupe: the worker consults the latest // SUCCESSFUL row for a normalized IP before submitting again. Never stores the API key. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AbuseReport.cs | Persistent log of reports sent to AbuseIPDB. Used by the eventual AbuseIPDB client to // honour the public API's rate limit (one report per IP per 15 minutes) and to surface // historical reports in the Configurator. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/ActiveBlock.cs | Currently installed firewall block. One row per (Provider, Ip) pair lets the eventual // AutoBlockWorker reconcile DB intent with provider state, retry failed installs, and // drive scheduled unblocks at ExpiresUtc. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/ActiveBlockStatus.cs | Health state of a single ActiveBlock row, used by the eventual AutoBlockWorker to drive // retries and to surface a row's operational status in the Configurator. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/Address.cs | IP reputation and aggregated activity counters per source address. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/Alert.cs | Output of an alert rule evaluation; persisted and surfaced via IPC. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AlertSeverity.cs | Severity classification for generated alerts. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AttackStat.cs | Materialised per-IP attack statistics. Acts as a denormalised summary so the future // Firewall stats UI can render dashboards without aggregating RawEvents on every refresh. // Populated by a future projection worker (Stage 3+); the entity ships in Stage 2 so // migrations align with the Stage 3 worker landing. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AttackStatProjection.cs | Pure helper for AttackStat projection: serialises the top-10 attempted logins list to // JSON (and back) using a deterministic format, computes the active-window duration, and // centralises every code path that writes AttackStat columns so the format does not drift. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AttackStatRowFormatter.cs | Pure clipboard formatters for the Stage 6B Configurator Attack Statistics tab. Multiline // output is human-readable for incident notes; TSV output stays on a single row with a // stable header for paste into Excel / SOC ticket grids. Lifted into Core so the // serialisations are unit-testable without WinForms. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AttackStatRowsExportFormatter.cs | Scope filter and export entry point for the RDP Activity tab "Export" submenu (Export // Selected / Red / Yellow / All Rows, each offering JSON / TXT / Markdown / CSV). Classifies rows // by the authoritative ThreatLevel the service returned, deliberately excludes the unresolved-IP // sentinel from the Red / Yellow scopes while counting it so the exclusion is never silent, and // projects AttackStatEntryDto onto the shared column set. All encoders live in TabularExportWriter. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/TabularExportWriter.cs | The single writer for tabular exports in the whole Configurator: document shape (source, scope, // provenance, columns, rows as ordered arrays) plus the JSON / TXT / Markdown / CSV encoders, CSV // quoting, Markdown escaping, cell sanitising, the extension-to-format mapping and file naming. // One implementation so a value with a comma, a quote or a line break is encoded identically // whichever grid produced the file. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AttackStatsAggregator.cs | Pure (DB-agnostic) projection from raw logon-event samples and active-block lookups into // one AttackStat row per source IP. Lifted out of the worker so it can be unit tested // without an EF Core DbContext. The worker is responsible for streaming inputs out of the // database; this helper does the deterministic projection / scoring. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AttackThreatLevel.cs | Cameyo / rdpmon-style threat-level classification used by the Attack Statistics tab. // Ordinals are append-only and mirror green / yellow / red dashboard semantics. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AttackThreatScoring.cs | Pure, deterministic helper that computes the Attack Statistics ThreatScore (0..100) and // classifies a row into the cameyo-style Green / Yellow / Red severity bands. Centralised // here so the projection worker, IPC handlers, UI, and tests all share one definition and // cannot drift. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/AuthAttemptFact.cs | Atomic, persisted authentication-attempt fact — the single source of truth for every // Total / Successful / Failed counter surfaced by Attack Statistics, IP facts, and the // Configurator dashboards (Detect_Attack_Strategy_v3.md, sections 8.1 and 17.14). Every // row is derived from exactly one Windows authentication-bearing event (Security 4624 / // 4625 / 4648 / 4768 / 4769 / 4771 / 4776 — never from RdpCoreTS / TCP / WTS); IpFact and // UserIpFact counters MUST be aggregations of this table. The v3 invariant: rebuilding // facts from scratch always yields identical counters. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/BlocklistEntry.cs | Persistent manual or automatic blocklist record. A row matches an inbound logon attempt // when its Ip and/or Login match. Rows are auditable, reversible, and reference the alert // that produced them (when produced automatically). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/BlocklistSource.cs | Identifies the origin of a BlocklistEntry / WhitelistEntry / LoginRule record so the // source of truth (manual operator action vs automatic rule evaluation vs external feed) // is auditable across upgrades. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/Bookmark.cs | Persisted EventLogWatcher bookmark for a single channel. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/ConnectionFactRowProjection.cs | Pure mapping helpers that derive Stage IP-E display strings from the fact-augmented // DTOs (AttackStatEntryDto, RdpSessionDto) without taking a WinForms dependency. The // Configurator row view-models call these helpers so the formatting rules are unit-testable // from RdpAudit.Core.Tests without referencing the WinForms host. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/CrossHostChain.cs | Persistent cross-host attack-chain row: stable ChainKey, rule, normalized user, risk score, failed-attempt count, first/last seen and linked AlertId (ack gate). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/CrossHostFact.cs | Persistent projection of one auth-attempt fact contributed by an agent host (kind, event id, normalized user, source IP, host-qualified time, stable DedupKey). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/CrossHostHostState.cs | Per-agent-host poll state: LastSeenUtc watermark and LastError; a missing row means the host has never been polled. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/CrossHostLink.cs | Persistent ordered hop of a CrossHostChain (host, kind, event id, time, source IP, user). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/DbProp.cs | Generic key-value store persisted alongside the audit database. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/EventCollectionAudit.cs | Persists SOC2-relevant changes to event collection and retention settings. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/EventDeletionLedger.cs | Append-only tombstone of removed raw-event evidence: one row accounts for the exact row count deleted for one code inside one UTC window, the trigger, the rule version, the reason and the initiator. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/EventDeletionScope.cs | Scope of an audited deletion: Global for a host-wide pass, PerIp when one source address was scoped. Ordinals are append-only. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/EventDeletionTrigger.cs | Cause of an audited deletion: DatabaseBytes, RawEventRows, FreeDiskBytes, AgeWindow, OperatorRequest. Ordinals are append-only. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/EventEnablement.cs | Stores an explicit enabled or disabled override for a Windows event identifier. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/EventRetention.cs | Stores a per-event retention override consumed by the retention worker. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/IngestionSequence.cs | Holds the durable monotonic ingestion sequence sidecar used by transactional event writers. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/IpEventSummary.cs | Stores durable per-IP event totals and first/last event evidence for efficient IP investigation. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/IpEventTypeCounter.cs | Stores a durable counter for each canonical IP and Windows event identifier pair. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/LoginRule.cs | Login trip-wire rule. Any attempted logon using a matching login name immediately // blocks the source IP. Use sparingly for honeypot logins such as `administrator` on a // host that does not use that account. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/OperationLog.cs | Durable record of an action the program performed — bans, firewall add/remove, registry / // policy changes, service install / start / stop / repair, settings changes, cleanup, // diagnostics runs, IPC command outcomes, background-job progress and failures, purge / // maintenance. This is deliberately SEPARATE from the security / attack event log // (RawEvent / Alert): it is the operator-facing audit trail of what RdpAudit itself did, // surfaced concisely in normal mode and with full detail (exception type / message / stack // and a free-form details JSON) when DEBUG mode is enabled. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/OperationLogSeverity.cs | Severity classification for durable operation-log entries (program actions, not security // attack events). Ordinals are append-only and persisted as integers, so existing values // must never be reused or reordered. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/RawEvent.cs | Persists a normalized Windows event and its durable ingestion metadata. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/RdpConnectionFact.cs | Durable, normalized historical fact for one RDP connection — the merged timeline of // TS-RCM 1149, TS-LSM 21/22/24/25 and Security 4624/4625 evidence keyed by LogonId or by // (WtsSessionId, UserName), enriched with the resolved source IP. Drives historical // Remote RDP Clients and Attack Statistics views without needing to re-mine RawEvents. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/Session.cs | Tracks the lifecycle of a single RDP session. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/SessionIpCorrelation.cs | Durable session-to-IP correlation fact. Persists the IP observed for a given // LogonId / (WtsSessionId, UserName) / UserName so the Remote RDP Clients view and // the in-memory correlation cache can resolve source IPs across service restarts // without re-mining 24h of RawEvents on every query. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/SessionStatus.cs | Lifecycle state of an RDP session. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/UnresolvedIpReason.cs | Reason taxonomy for why a logon-relevant event carried no usable source IP. Lets the // Attack Statistics tab group the unresolved sentinel row by *why* it is unresolved rather // than presenting one opaque bucket. Pure / DB-agnostic so it can be unit tested and shared // by the normalizer, the aggregator, and the Configurator UI without an EF Core context. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Models/WhitelistEntry.cs | Persistent IP whitelist record. Whitelisted IPs always bypass automatic blocking even // when other rules would match. The table is intentionally small and indexed for fast // membership lookups from the future AutoBlockWorker. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/RestApi/IPagedEventReader.cs | Abstraction over the bounded, read-only page query answered by the REST /events endpoint. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/RestApi/PagedEventPage.cs | Result model returned by IPagedEventReader for /events: one bounded page of events plus the total count and the optional EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN digest. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/RestApi/PagedEventRow.cs | Serializable projection of one RawEvent row for the /events endpoint. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/RestApi/PagedEventsQuery.cs | Normalized, clamped query contract for the read-only REST /events endpoint; malformed time bounds and over-long text filters are rejected up front. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Retention/RetentionCodeKey.cs | Identity of an information-density retention code: event id, channel and optional 16-byte source address key, plus the deterministic IP-hex tie-break and the operator-facing label. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Retention/RetentionCodeState.cs | Bounded snapshot of one retention code - total rows, anchor, oldest protected tail row, exact eligible-interior density, surrogate-key span and the offered candidate rows - that the planner reasons about without touching the database. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Retention/RetentionPlan.cs | Planner result contract: ordered deletion steps carrying the code, the row count, the window and both protection boundaries; the honest skip list with reasons; the rule version; and the aggregate planned row count. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Retention/InformationDensityRetentionPlanner.cs | Pure deterministic planner for information-density retention: anchor and tail protection, age floor, eligibility threshold, protected classes, legal hold, density-ordered budget distribution and total-order tie-breaks. No I/O, so every invariant is unit-testable and two identical inputs always produce an identical plan. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Security/DpapiSecretProtector.cs | Windows DPAPI-backed implementation of ISecretProtector. Uses LocalMachine scope by // default so the SYSTEM service account and an administrative configurator can both // read the same envelope. Plaintext never leaves managed memory in cleartext form // beyond the immediate call site. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Security/ISecretProtector.cs | Abstraction for protecting / unprotecting configuration secrets at rest. Producers store // a protected envelope (JSON object with "$protected" key) inside appsettings.json; the // service unprotects the envelope at runtime when a secret is actually needed. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Security/InMemorySecretProtector.cs | Deterministic, non-production ISecretProtector used for unit tests and non-Windows CI. // Wraps the plaintext in the protected-envelope JSON format but stores it XOR'd with a // fixed key so round-tripping is testable without DPAPI / OS dependencies. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Security/ProtectedEnvelope.cs | Helpers for the JSON envelope format used to wrap protected secrets in appsettings.json. // Envelope shape: { "$protected": "<base64-cipher>", "scope": "LocalMachine" }. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Security/SecretProtectionException.cs | Exception raised by ISecretProtector implementations on protect/unprotect failures. // The message is curated: it never contains plaintext values, envelope bytes, or stack data. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Security/WebTokenVault.cs | DPAPI machine-scope token vault used by the WebUI proxy: atomically persists a CSPRNG bearer token under %ProgramData%\RdpAudit\WebUI\secrets\token.dat with an idempotent SYSTEM+Administrators ACL (optional ReadAndExecute for the IIS pool identity). Refuses wrong-ACL / corrupt files, rotates tokens on demand, and never logs plaintext. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/WebUiPublisher.cs | Publishes the WebUI static wwwroot into %ProgramData%\RdpAudit\WebUI: per-file atomic copy (tmp+move) with SHA-256 verification before and after the move, manifest-driven skip/stale-removal, refusal of a source secrets folder, and an optional ReadAndExecute grant for the IIS pool that is verified before any content lands. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/WebUiPoolAcl.cs | Shared helper that grants the IIS application-pool identity ReadAndExecute on a target folder (app root / secrets) and verifies the grant; one canonical implementation consumed by both the publisher and the app stager so the ACL never drifts between them. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/WebUiAppStager.cs | Stages the complete published RdpAudit.Web application output (web.config mandatory, runtime + wwwroot) into %ProgramData%\RdpAudit\WebUI for in-process IIS hosting (plan variant A): per-file atomic copy (tmp+move) with SHA-256 verification on both sides, idempotent manifest, stale cleanup, refusal of a source secrets folder, and an optional verified ReadAndExecute grant for the IIS application pool. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/WebUiCertificateSource.cs | Finds an existing usable web-UI HTTPS certificate (LoopbackBindingInformation host, validity, private key) and builds the deterministic self-signed certificate PowerShell command invoked when none exists; never leaks private material to logs. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/WebUiAppSettingsEditor.cs | Atomic read-modify-write editor for the staged RdpAudit.Web appsettings.json and the service appsettings.json: safely sets the Api.Enabled/Token envelope and the WebUiProxy section while preserving all other JSON content, with tmp+move writes and never emitting plaintext tokens. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/IisConfigureCommandSet.cs | Pure command catalog for the Configure IIS workflow (AppCmd site/app-pool/modules, netsh http sslcert, sc.exe stop/start): deterministic, locale-safe argument builders and curated stdout classifiers (ANCM presence, site existence) that never execute anything. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/IisInstallCommandSet.cs | Pure feature catalog, status snapshot and deterministic Install IIS command builders for the Web Access tab: the static-file feature ids, the DISM enable-feature names for that set, the separate IIS management-tools set (appcmd.exe) and the argument vector built from any subset. The Windows PowerShell-only ServerManager string builders were removed - nothing could execute them outside 5.1 compatibility and the product installs through DISM. Nothing here executes, fully unit-testable. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/IisStatusProbe.cs | Read-only fail-closed IIS status probe: InetStp registry fast path + DISM /English /Get-FeatureInfo confirmation via the centralized command runner, W3SVC service existence check, and published index.html detection. IIisPlatformProbe is the stub seam; the probe never changes system state and reports unknown instead of inventing facts without rights. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/IWebAccessWorkflowHost.cs | Contract between the Web Access tab and the IIS workflows (Step 10 Install / Step 11 Configure), plus the IisInstallRunOutcome it returns: the curated status line, one result per IisInstallPlan item (what the progress window paints) and the success / already-installed verdict. RunInstallIisAsync takes an IProgress<IisInstallStepResult> sink and a cancellation token, and never throws - an unavailable component is reported as a failed or skipped item. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/IisInstallPlan.cs | Pure, IO-free install plan behind "Install IIS": the ordered IisInstallStepId items (elevation, IIS feature set, IIS management tools, ASP.NET Core Hosting Bundle, verification), their operator-readable titles and descriptions, and the IisInstallStepResult / IisStepOutcome shapes the progress window renders. Single source of truth for both the list the operator sees before the run and the sequence the workflow executes, so the two can never drift. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/HostingBundleCommandSet.cs | Command shapes and read-only detection for the ASP.NET Core Hosting Bundle, the only source of the native AspNetCoreModuleV2 that in-process hosting of the published dashboard requires (no DISM feature provides it). Holds the pinned winget package id and the unattended install argument vector (the same channel install.ps1 already uses) plus AspNetCoreModuleProbe, which reports the module present only when both native binaries are on disk and otherwise names the missing ones. Indication only - the authoritative check stays the appcmd module listing in Configure IIS; nothing here executes a command. | Step 11 Configure): the page delegates the run and renders a curated fail-closed one-line outcome; the host is injectable so the page is testable with a fake. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/WebUi/WebAccessUIModel.cs | Pure, UI-agnostic state model for the Web Access tab: IWebAccessPreferenceStore abstraction, WebAccessUIState projection and WebAccessUIStateFactory.Build. Encodes the phase-gated management checkbox, workflow busy/locked flags, and WorkflowButtonsEnabled so the enablement contract is unit-testable without WinForms. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/RawEventDecimationEngine.cs | Implements the optional frequency-oriented decimation pass for raw event telemetry as pure parameterised SQLite SQL over the shared AuditDbContext. One call fully decimates the selected candidates; disabled / no-candidate / fresh-row / NULL-SourceIp cases are no-ops. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/BlittableEquality.cs | Allocation-free structural equality and hashing for the packed, blittable shard // structs, so their on-disk representation is the single source of truth for equality. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/ChannelCodeMap.cs | Converts canonical event channel names into compact shard channel codes. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/Crc32C.cs | Hardware-accelerated CRC-32C (Castagnoli, polynomial 0x1EDC6F41) used to detect // torn writes and bit rot in shard headers and records. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/ShardHeader.cs | Double-buffered, CRC-validated shard file header. Torn-write survivable: a partial // crash during header flip is detected by CRC mismatch on the active copy, and the // reader falls back to the inactive copy. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/ShardPath.cs | Canonicalises a source IP into a safe relative path under the actions/ root. // Rejects traversal, reserved DOS names, ADS, UNC prefixes; never derives the file // name from raw event text. IPv6 is normalised to hex without colons. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/ShardReader.cs | Read-only, memory-mapped, zero-copy iterator over a shard file's live records. // Detects header generation change and re-snapshots so a concurrent Commit() from // the writer never produces a torn view. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/ShardRecord.cs | Fixed 128-byte on-disk record layout for the per-IP shard file. Cache-line aligned, // reinterpret-castable from a memory-mapped Span<byte>, no managed references. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Storage/Sharding/ShardWriter.cs | Zero-allocation appender for a per-IP shard file. Ring semantics: on overflow the // oldest record is O(1) evicted by advancing the head pointer; no rewrite or compaction // pause. Header is double-buffered and CRC-guarded so a torn write is always detectable. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Util/ActiveRdpSessionClassifier.cs | v1.2.2 — pure classifier deciding whether a parsed qwinsta / quser row should be // treated as the operator-visible "current / active RDP" session. The previous // implementation propagated the raw qwinsta ">" marker straight into the DTO's // IsCurrent field, which under LocalSystem (the Service runs as) points at // session 0 ("services") — so the Remote RDP Clients tab attributed Current? to a // system row while the real RDP session went unflagged. The validated semantics: // State == Active // AND SessionName starts with "rdp-tcp#" (OrdinalIgnoreCase) // AND UserName non-empty // AND 1 < SessionId < 65536 // The classifier is intentionally side-effect-free; the Configurator-side and // Service-side enumeration paths both consume it. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Util/ActiveRdpTcpEnricher.cs | Pure live-TCP enricher used as a last-resort fallback when the DB-backed // LocalSessionEnricher cannot supply a Client IP for an Active RDP session // (typical when SessionCorrelationCache is empty, e.g. fresh install). Given: // * the set of currently-active RDP user sessions (RdpSessionDto); // * the set of currently-established TCP endpoints on the configured RDP port; // the enricher assigns a remote IP ONLY when both sides are unambiguous — // exactly one active session and exactly one non-loopback / non-wildcard // established endpoint. Multi-session / multi-endpoint situations are left // untouched (Client IP remains blank) so the operator is never lied to. The // enricher never overwrites a session that already has a ClientAddress set and // never touches disconnected / listener / non-RDP rows. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Util/ActiveRdpTcpProvider.cs | Queries currently-established TCP connections terminating at a specific local port // (the dynamically-resolved RDP listener port). Uses the managed // IPGlobalProperties.GetActiveTcpConnections() API so no P/Invoke is required — the // managed call already wraps GetExtendedTcpTable under the hood and returns a fully // typed list including state. Filters down to Established connections whose local // port matches the RDP listener and projects every match into ActiveRdpTcpEndpoint. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Util/ActiveSessionCounter.cs | Pure counter that derives the "Active RDP sessions" KPI from the same // RdpSessionDto stream the Remote RDP Clients tab consumes. Centralizing the // rule prevents the Overview dashboard and the Sessions tab from disagreeing // (e.g. Overview showing 0 while the Sessions tab lists two active rows). A // session is counted when it is in the Active state AND attached to a real // user (Listener / Services / Console rows are excluded so the KPI reflects // actual logged-on remote clients). |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Util/AddressListFilter.cs | Pure, UI-agnostic predicate used by the Configurator Firewall tab to filter // blocklist / whitelist / active-block / login-rule grids by a free-text search. // Lifted into Core so the matching rules can be unit tested without a UI host. |
| src/RdpAudit.Core/Util/AttackStatsFilter.cs | Pure, UI-agnostic predicate for the Configurator Attack Statistics tab. Mirrors the // server-side AttackStatsRequest filter so the UI can pre-filter cached rows without // another IPC round-trip while operators type. Lifted into Core so the rules can be unit // tested without a UI host. |
