| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/FirewallBlockScopeContractTests.cs | Pins the persisted contract behind the Firewall "Block scope" dropdown. The Configurator // writes (int)FirewallBlockScope into config ("Firewall:BlockScope") and the Service binds // it straight back to FirewallOptions.BlockScope, so these enum ordinals are an on-disk ABI: // reordering or renumbering them would silently flip a saved "RDP port only" selection into // "All inbound" (or vice versa) on the next service read. These tests guard the exact int // values the dropdown options map to, and the backward-compatible default (AllInbound). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/FirewallContractTests.cs | Validates invariants on the firewall abstraction DTOs and provider-kind enum. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/FirewallDiagnosticsReportBuilderTests.cs | Unit tests for FirewallDiagnosticsReportBuilder — pinning the sections an operator relies // on to confirm RdpAudit's IP blocking is actually enforced (resolved RDP port, RdpAudit // group rules, per-provider availability, alternate backends, third-party interference) and // the reconciliation warning that fires when active-block rows exceed verified enforcement. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/FirewallDiagnosticsScannerBackendTests.cs | Verifies the diagnostics report surfaces (a) which firewall-scanner backend was used plus // the manual-equivalent PowerShell command, (b) a locale-risk warning when the fragile // netsh text backend was used, (c) honest third-party (Kaspersky) wording that never claims // "no interference" when a third-party firewall is detected and never blames it when the // scanner itself was the failing path, and (d) clearly-labeled enforcement count buckets so // an enabled-blocklist vs per-IP mismatch is explained rather than alarming. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/FirewallProviderClassifierTests.cs | Pure-classifier coverage for FirewallProviderClassifier and the diagnostics text // formatter. Verifies that Kaspersky-only signals classify as KasperskyDetected, // KSWS-style firewall management promotes to KasperskyManagedWindowsFirewall, and // third-party services classify into ThirdPartyFirewallUnknown without false-positive // Kaspersky labelling. Built without any Win32 / WMI dependency so it runs from any host. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/FirewallProviderRoutingTests.cs | Unit tests for FirewallProviderRouting — the pure mapping from provider kind + local // enforcement backend to the stable IFirewallProvider.ProviderId string. Guarantees the // auto-block and expiration workers agree on which provider services a block so a row is // unblocked by the same provider that installed it. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/GridValueComparerTests.cs | Unit tests for GridValueComparer — pinning the type-aware ordering (IP, DateTime, duration, // numeric, string) used by the Configurator's sortable grids so columns sort naturally rather // than lexicographically, and blanks sort last. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/InMemorySecretProtectorTests.cs | Round-trip tests for the non-production InMemorySecretProtector. Covers envelope // detection, protect/unprotect parity, and pass-through of unwrapped plaintext values. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/IpClassifierTests.cs | Validates IpClassifier coverage for RFC1918 and special-purpose ranges. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/IpEventsExportFormatterTests.cs | Stage A — locks the Export-All-IP-Events formatter contracts in JSON / TXT / Markdown // / CSV. | tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/IpNormalizerTests.cs | v1.2.1 stabilisation gate — pins the canonical-form contract of IpNormalizer so // punctuation-wrapped, port-suffixed, IPv6-zone-tagged, and IPv4-mapped-IPv6 values // collapse to the single textual key Attack-Statistics / RDP-Clients aggregates rely // on. The real-host bug report was a 4776 NTLM failure where Live Events showed the // attacker as ".77.37.192.246" but the Attack Statistics row appeared under // (unresolved) because the previous normalizer rejected the leading-dot form. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/IpReportabilityTests.cs | Validates the centralized IpReportability classifier across IPv4 private/public, IPv6 // link-local / loopback / unique-local / multicast, CGNAT, documentation/test, reserved, // invalid, unresolved, and whitelisted inputs. Covers the live-RDP diagnostics fixtures // named in the brief (77.37.192.246 public, 192.168.x private, fe80::/10 link-local). |

Also pins the canonical URL formats so accidental template changes // are caught immediately. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/IpcCommandStabilityTests.cs | Locks append-only IPC and result-status ordinals against breaking ABI changes. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/IpcConstantsTimeoutTests.cs | Locks the per-command IPC round-trip deadline policy that fixed the historic // "service not running" misreport: long-running firewall / diagnostics commands MUST // get the long (60 s) deadline so the client and server never abandon a request the // service is still completing, while cheap read-only commands keep the short default. // Both ends of a round-trip consult IpcConstants.TimeoutMsFor, so this single map is the // contract — if it regresses (e.g. a Repair command drops back to 5 s) the bug returns. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/LiveEventFilterTests.cs | Stage 4 — covers the LiveEvents grid filter predicate. Each field is exercised in // isolation, then combinations check AND semantics. Time-range bounds are validated at // and around their boundaries. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/LiveEventRowFormatterTests.cs | Stage 4 — locks the LiveEvents copy-to-clipboard serialisation. Multiline output is // assertable line-by-line; TSV output stays on a single row with a stable header. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/LocalSessionEnricherTests.cs | Covers the pure local-fallback enricher used by the Configurator when the service // IPC is unreachable. Validates the documented match order: SessionIpCorrelation by // (WtsSessionId, UserName), then RdpConnectionFact by (WtsSessionId, UserName), then // unambiguous recent RdpConnectionFact by UserName. Listener / console / services // rows must not be enriched. Authoritative live values must never be overwritten. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/LocalTimeFormatterTests.cs | v1.2.1 — pin the local-time rendering contract used by every operator-facing grid / // status strip / diagnostic block. The DB remains UTC; the rendering boundary MUST // produce a local-clock string so the operator sees timestamps that match the wall // clock they are looking at. Assertions are timezone-agnostic: we compare against // the same conversion the helper performs so the tests work in any CI timezone. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/LogonTypeCatalogTests.cs | Locks the LogonTypeCatalog decoding contract: well-known Windows logon type codes // decode to their canonical short names, unknown codes fall back gracefully without // throwing, and the inline formatter produces a stable, non-null tooltip string // (including a dedicated message for a null code). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/LogsOptionsTests.cs | Locks the operation-log viewing-depth and page-size policy: the 60-day default that // bounds the Logs tab and retention pass, the [1, 3650] depth clamp, the [1, 1000] page-size // clamp, and the "0 or negative means use the default" semantics the IPC handler relies on. // If these regress, the Logs tab could ask the service to scan or serialize the whole table. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/MikroTikSetupCommandsTests.cs | Locks the MikroTik RouterOS v7 setup command bundle exposed on the Configurator // MikroTik tab: every required step is present, every placeholder is preserved, the // bundle is non-empty, every line is CRLF-terminated, and — critically — no plaintext // secret value is embedded anywhere (the password is always represented by the // PasswordPlaceholder token, never a literal). These tests guarantee that the // "Copy commands" button on the MikroTik tab will paste a ready-to-edit template into // the operator's terminal without ever leaking a credential. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SecurityAuditTests.cs | Locks the security audit: 37 catalogue entries with unique ids and the reference weights, each rule // family firing on a fabricated snapshot, an unreadable menu becoming S-00 while an absent package stays // silent, and — critically — the SNMP community string, the script password and the note text never // reaching the evidence or the rendered Markdown. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/MikroTikUrlBuilderTests.cs | Pure URL composition tests for the Stage 9 MikroTik integration. | tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/MonitoringConfigRepairTests.cs | Stage 2 — ensures stale appsettings.json (pre-v3 config that dropped the Security // channel or wrote a partial EnabledEventIds filter) is repaired in place when the // service materialises MonitoringOptions. Without this fix the live Security watcher // is never armed on upgrade and the Configurator UI reports Failed=0 even when // PowerShell can see Security 4625 events. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/NetshRuleScannerTests.cs | Verifies the rule-by-port scanner so the "Windows Firewall RDP rule present" probe // stays localisation-tolerant and uses the configured RDP port rather than the // well-known 3389 — matching the new contract laid out in feedback (no localized // Windows Firewall group name lookups). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/NtStatusFormatterTests.cs | Covers NtStatusFormatter — canonicalises the three textual forms Windows writes for // NTSTATUS values (hex 0xXXXXXXXX, unsigned decimal, signed decimal int32) into one // stable form so SubStatusCatalog lookups and EF/SQL predicates align across producers // and OS builds. Pins the user-reported real-host values from PowerShell evidence: // Status / SubStatus rendered as signed decimal int32 such as -1073741715 (bad password). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/OemConsoleEncodingTests.cs | Stage 4 — verifies the OEM-code-page resolver used to decode redirected // sc.exe stdout/stderr without mojibake. Tests pin specific code pages so // the assertions do not depend on the host's culture and can therefore run // on Linux CI as well as on a Russian / French / English Windows box. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/PerEventIpResolverPositionalFallbackTests.cs | Cameyo rdpmon compatibility — verifies PerEventIpResolver applies the positional // Properties[19] fallback for Security 4625 and Properties[12] fallback for 4648 only // when the named IpAddress field is absent, and never overrides a valid named IpAddress. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/PerEventIpResolverTests.cs | Verifies PerEventIpResolver extracts the correct IP field per (Channel, EventId) and // rejects hostname-only events (no NetBIOS name should ever land in SourceIp). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/PowerShellFirewallRuleParserTests.cs | Verifies the locale-independent PowerShell JSON firewall-rule parser. The whole point of // this path is that ConvertTo-Json property names are English-stable on every host UI // culture (unlike netsh text labels), and that rules are recognised by group=RdpAudit as // well as by rule-name prefix. These tests pin: array vs single-object JSON shapes, // group / display-group matching, name-prefix matching, RemoteAddress /32 + range + array // equivalence, and enum-as-string vs enum-as-integer tolerance. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ProtectedEnvelopeTests.cs | Validates the JSON envelope helper used to wrap protected configuration secrets. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/QuserParserTests.cs | Validates QuserParser against the operator's exact field-reported sample (English // STATE tokens from a Russian-locale Windows host via chcp 437) and the typical // quser output shapes — including the "current session" marker, missing // SESSIONNAME column, and trailing IDLE TIME / LOGON TIME columns. The pure parser // is the canonical input to QwinstaQuserMerger, so its column-attribution rules // are exercised exhaustively here. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/QwinstaConsoleEncodingTests.cs | Pins the console-encoding policy now owned by OemConsoleEncoding and reached through the historical QwinstaConsoleEncoding name: cp866 decodes the Russian Cyrillic state tokens without mojibake while the pre-fix UTF-8 decode is shown to produce it, cp437 is selected for English hosts, and an unresolvable or negative code page falls back to cp437 rather than to UTF-8 (the two helpers used to disagree exactly there). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/QwinstaParserTests.cs | Validates the QwinstaParser against representative qwinsta / query-session // outputs, including the current-session marker, header variants, disconnected // rows that are missing the session-name column, and the Russian-language // (Cyrillic) output reported in the field when the operator's console runs // under a non-English Windows UI culture. The parser is header-agnostic when no // English header is present so the operator still sees their live RDP sessions // on localized hosts. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/QwinstaQuserMergerTests.cs | Validates the QwinstaQuserMerger using the operator's exact field-reported // combination of qwinsta + quser output: qwinsta emits a row with blank SESSIONNAME // for user "af" id 2 Disc, plus rdp-tcp#26 for user "md" id 3 Active; quser reports // both users with id/state matching qwinsta. The merger must fill the blank // SessionName on the af row using quser data, and must NOT add any new rows or // touch the rdp-tcp#26 row. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/QwinstaSessionMapperTests.cs | Locks the QwinstaSessionMapper that converts pure parser rows into // RdpSessionDto instances used by both the service-side and Configurator-side // session-listing paths. Guarantees state normalisation, IsActive / IsDisconnected // derivation, IsActiveRdp calculation and raw-query-current propagation remain // stable while the operator-scoped IsCurrent flag is owned by CurrentRdpSessionMatcher. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RawEventDecimationEngineTests.cs | Unit tests for the RawEventDecimationEngine against in-memory SQLite: disabled no-op, burst head/tail preservation, single-tick convergence, batch chunking, candidate threshold / cap, NULL SourceIp, fresh-row protection, and rare-burst survival. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/InformationDensityRetentionPlannerTests.cs | Pure planner tests: anchor and tail survival for hostile inputs, interior-only candidates, age floor, eligibility threshold, protected classes, legal hold, density ordering, deterministic tie-breaks, budget distribution, over-claim guard, determinism and idempotency. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpAuditFirewallRuleMatcherTests.cs | v1.3.8 — pin the firewall rule reconciliation semantics so verification recognises // BOTH identity forms Windows Firewall presents for an RdpAudit block, and reports // duplicate / canonicalization status. Fixtures mirror the affected-host evidence: the // blocked IP 62.176.5.200 surfaced as a canonical rule (Name == RdpAudit-Block-<ip>, // Group RdpAudit) AND a GUID-named rule (Name == {GUID}, DisplayName == RdpAudit-Block-<ip>, // empty Group). Both must verify enforcement; both must be reported as a duplicate pair. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpAuditPathsTests.cs | Locks the D2 single-source-of-truth path layout. RdpAuditPaths must resolve every // canonical runtime path under an explicit root (a temp folder in tests) so the test // suite never constructs, creates or inspects the real %ProgramData%\RdpAudit tree. // Also pins DiagnosticLogRotation: append-restart on the size cap, exact newline // handling, and directory auto-create. No test here may touch the machine's // CommonApplicationData folder. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpAuditVersionMetadataTests.cs | Pins the current release version to exactly 2.0.2 across every assembly metadata // surface that publish.ps1 and the running Service surface to the operator: the // AssemblyInformationalVersion (the SemVer driving the Service tab "Runtime version" // line), AssemblyVersion / FileVersion (the four-part identifiers embedded in the // PE file and surfaced by FileVersionInfo), plus a hard guard against the previous // 1.0.0 placeholder default and the 1.5.x stream leaking back into the build. // If the release stream advances, this test must move with it - never weaken the // assertion to "starts with". |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpConfigurationEditModelTests.cs | Locks the pure edit-model behaviour that backs the editable RDP Configuration tab — // DTO-to-model mapping, validation (port range / enum bounds), and the deterministic // change-set diff that the downstream writer turns into registry mutations. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpConfigurationModelTests.cs | Stage RDP-1 — locks the pure RDP configuration helpers (port validation, enum mapping, // enabled-flag interpretation). Keeps the new RDP Configuration tab deterministic across // registry-value edge cases. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpConfigurationSnapshotServiceTests.cs | Locks the two-tier read strategy used by the "RDP Configuration" tab — IPC first, // local fallback when IPC returns null / throws / reports a non-success status. The // orchestrator lives in RdpAudit.Core.Util, accepts plain delegates, and is tested // here without a live named pipe and without any dependency on the Windows registry. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpListenerPortResolverTests.cs | Validates the pure ClassifyRaw branch of RdpListenerPortResolver. The // Resolve() entry point reads HKLM and is Windows-only, so it is exercised // through the pure helper here — every meaningful path (registry value present // and valid; missing; out of range) maps to a deterministic outcome. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpServiceAccountCatalogTests.cs | Locks the RdpServiceAccountCatalog contract: well-known built-in principals and the // per-session DWM-/UMFD- families and machine ($) accounts are recognised and described, // ordinary human usernames return null, the legend covers the documented accounts, and // the inline annotator round-trips ordinary names unchanged. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RdpSessionFallbackOrchestratorTests.cs | Locks the two-tier session-list strategy used by the Remote RDP Clients tab — IPC // first, local fallback when IPC returns null / throws / reports a non-success status. // Mirrors the read-side orchestrator used by the RDP Configuration tab; ensures the // page never just reports "service unreachable" — it always falls back to local rows // whenever the fallback succeeds. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/RouteBlackholeCommandBuilderTests.cs | Unit tests for the experimental route-blackhole command builder: destination / gateway // validation, gateway classification (the gate that prevents forwarding attacker traffic // to a live next-hop), and the route add / delete / print argument vectors. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ScCommandBuilderTests.cs | Verifies the sc.exe argument shape returned by ScCommandBuilder. sc.exe // exit 1639 (ERROR_INVALID_COMMAND_LINE) reproduces when the option name // and its value are passed as a single argv token (e.g. "binPath= C:\Path"). // These tests pin the contract that the builder emits the option name // (ending with '=') and its value as separate argv elements, which is the // only shape sc.exe parses reliably under ProcessStartInfo.ArgumentList. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ScDeleteOutputFormatterTests.cs | Stage 4 — locks the English-fallback behaviour of the sc.exe delete output // formatter. The Service tab uninstall dialog must always show a readable // English banner, regardless of operator locale or whether the captured sc.exe // output decoded correctly. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ServiceButtonStateModelTests.cs | Stage 2 — locks the Service tab button-state mapping against the authoritative // SCM snapshot plus the installed/distribution binary comparison. Install is gated // on a usable distribution, Start/Stop reflect the live SCM state, Update only // enables when the distribution differs from the installed binary, transitioning // states keep lifecycle buttons disabled. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ServiceDiagnosticsReportBuilderTests.cs | Locks the verdict resolution rules for the Service tab Copy diagnostics report. // Each test arranges a single concrete mismatch (or none) and asserts the resulting // ServiceDiagnosticsVerdict so the headline label the operator sees can never silently // regress to "OK" when the inputs are out of sync. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ServiceInstallationInfoTests.cs | Stage 2 — locks the Win32_Service PathName / ImagePath parser. SCM emits the // executable path either bare or quoted with optional arguments appended, and the // Service tab needs the absolute exe path so it can fingerprint the installed // binary. Also covers the locale-stable IsRunning / IsTransitioning / IsPaused / // IsStopped helpers used by ServiceButtonStateModel. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ServiceLayoutTests.cs | Verifies that ServiceLayout.ResolveSiblingDistribution discovers the // published Service distribution folder regardless of where the user has // placed the publish/ root on disk. Assertions use Path.GetFullPath so the // expected values match the platform-specific path normalization performed // by DirectoryInfo.FullName (e.g. on Windows, rooted "\foo" is anchored to // the current drive). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ServiceQueryParserStateCodeTests.cs | Stage 4 — locks the locale-stable numeric state-code path used by the // Service tab to decide whether the Windows service is running. Localized // versions of "sc.exe queryex" rewrite the textual STATE token ("RUNNING" // becomes "РАБОТАЕТ" on Russian Windows, "EN COURS D'EXÉCUTION" on French // Windows, an ideographic phrase on Chinese Windows). The numeric code on // the same STATE line stays at 4 (SERVICE_RUNNING) regardless. These tests // assert that ServiceQueryParser extracts the numeric STATE code for each // of those locales and that ServiceQueryResult.IsRunning fires off the // numeric code rather than the textual name. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ServiceQueryParserTests.cs | Verifies the textual output parser used by the Configurator's Service tab // to extract the running service's STATE (name + numeric code) and PID from // "sc.exe queryex". The parser must tolerate locale-specific whitespace and // gracefully report "not installed" when sc.exe prints error 1060. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ServiceStateViewTests.cs | Stage 2 — locks the Service tab state aggregator. Covers every state model // transition the user-reported symptoms revealed: SCM running but IPC unreachable, // IPC connected but SCM not installed, installed-path-missing, distribution-missing, // update-available, running-older-binary, and the locale-stable Process line. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SessionCommandBuilderTests.cs | Validates the SessionCommandBuilder: session-id range checks, disconnect // and logoff argument shapes, and the three mstsc /shadow variants. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SessionConsoleCommandFactoryTests.cs | Validates the parse-stable English console spawn factory. The factory must emit // the exact "cmd.exe /d /c \"chcp 437 >nul & tool\"" shape for every trusted tool — // this is the technique that pins the active code page so qwinsta and quser produce // English STATE tokens regardless of UI culture. The arguments string is composed // entirely from constants in the factory, so shell injection from operator input // is impossible by construction. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ShadowGateDecisionTests.cs | Exercises the pure ShadowGate evaluator — covers the regression where the // Configurator refused mstsc /shadow even though the operator's manual // `mstsc /noConsentPrompt /control /admin /shadow:N` command works because the // OS Shadow policy in fact permits it. Verifies that local registry policy is // honoured when the service is unreachable or returns a stale refusal, and that // genuine denials still fall through. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ShadowPolicyModelTests.cs | Validates the pure ShadowPolicyModel — value classification, mode/permission // mapping and the "enable all permissions" preset constant. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Sharding/ShardWriterTests.cs | Verifies shard persistence, eviction, torn-header recovery, and append allocation behavior. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SqlLikeEscaperTests.cs | Pins the LIKE-pattern escaping contract that protects the username / IP / log search // boxes from LIKE-wildcard injection. Values still flow as SQL parameters via // EF.Functions.Like, so this guards the pattern semantics; the tests also exercise the // representative hostile login strings (SQL, command, CR/LF) to prove they are handled // as literal text rather than altering the match shape. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SqliteConcurrencyGuardsTests.cs | Guards the shared-cache ban in the shipped connection strings (write and read-only) and the transient-failure classifier, including the DbUpdateException wrapper that made the bounded retry dead code. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SqliteSupportBundleTests.cs | Pins the canonical SQLite diagnostic support bundle contract consumed by publish.ps1 // (Ensure-SqliteSupportBundle), the Configurator install/repair validation and the // Diagnostic-tab preflight. These tests guarantee the required-file set cannot silently // drift (count, exact leaf names, stable order) and that Verify / DescribeMissing behave // correctly for complete, partial and missing-directory inputs without throwing. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage2EnumStabilityTests.cs | Locks the ordinal values of Stage 2 enums (BlocklistSource, ActiveBlockStatus). These // ordinals are persisted to SQLite and must never be reused or reordered; the tests fail // if a future change reassigns existing values. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage2MigrationUpgradeTests.cs | Validates that Stage 2 EF Core migrations apply cleanly both on a fresh database and on // a Stage-1-only database. Confirms the Stage 2 migration is present, that the upgrade // path preserves Stage 1 rows, and that the down-migration removes Stage 2 tables. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage17EventDeletionLedgerSchemaTests.cs | Stage 17 migration schema tests: applies the whole migration chain to a temporary database, proves it reproduces the EF model table-for-table and column-for-column, and checks the ledger table, columns and indexes plus the Down path. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage2SchemaTests.cs | Validates Stage 2 EF Core mappings against an in-memory SQLite database. Confirms that // the new tables can be created from migrations, that unique indices reject duplicates, // and that the migration upgrade path leaves Stage 1 data untouched. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage4FirewallHardeningTests.cs | Stage 4 regression coverage for the firewall / Kaspersky diagnostics hardening: // * NetshRuleScanner now requires Enabled=Yes + Protocol=TCP and exposes diagnostic // helpers (ExplainPortMatches, EnumerateEnabledAllowInboundTcpPorts) so the // Prerequisites tab can explain stale-port / disabled-rule failures. // * LocalRulePolicyParser surfaces the `LocalFirewallRules N/A (GPO-store only)` row // from `netsh advfirewall show allprofiles`. // * FirewallProviderDiagnostics.BuildDiagnosticsText emits the per-profile // LocalFirewallRules rows + a clear GPO-store-only note. // * FirewallProviderClassifier still detects the user's reported `AVP21.24` Kaspersky // service through the `AVP` fragment, so service / CLI detection holds even when // SecurityCenter2 is absent (Windows Server SKUs). // User scenario (Windows 10 Pro 19045 ru-RU, resolved RDP port 55554, Kaspersky as // FirewallProduct / AntiVirusProduct) is reproduced verbatim in the integration-style // tests at the bottom. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage4SessionIpCorrelationSchemaTests.cs | Validates the Stage IP-B SessionIpCorrelations table — EF mapping, column nullability, // and the supporting indexes used by Remote RDP Clients lookup and maintenance retention. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage5RdpConnectionFactSchemaTests.cs | Validates the Stage IP-C RdpConnectionFacts table — EF mapping, column nullability, // default values, and the supporting indexes used by Remote RDP Clients historical // enrichment and Attack Statistics export. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/Stage6EventCorrectnessSchemaTests.cs | Validates the Stage 6 RawEvent schema change — adds SourceIpUnresolved with a // false default, applies cleanly on a fresh database and as an upgrade from Stage 5, // and preserves Stage 5 rows during the upgrade. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SubStatusCatalogTests.cs | Pins the v3 SubStatus translation table (Detect_Attack_Strategy_v3.md §3.1) so attack // classification can rely on stable, human-readable failure reasons (Bad Password, // No Such User, Account Locked Out, ...). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/SuccessfulSessionsExportFormatterTests.cs | Locks the SuccessfulSessionsExportFormatter contract across JSON / TXT / Markdown / // CSV: only genuinely successful sessions are included, non-successful facts (pure // failures) are excluded, and every rendered format carries the decision evidence // (the decoded event ids the success verdict was based on). Also locks the file-naming // and IsSuccessful predicate helpers. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ToolsDiagnosticsFormattingTests.cs | Pins the pure Tools Diag formatting / metadata mapping that the Configurator's Tools Diag // tab relies on. Exercises ToolProbeResultMapper (ExternalCommandResult -> ToolProbeResultDto, // the firewall BackendCommandAttempt projection, the skipped-probe shape and the locale hint) // and ToolsDiagnosticsReportBuilder (the copyable transcript). All inputs are fake runner / // attempt objects so the suite runs on Linux CI without ever spawning a Windows command. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/UnresolvedIpClassifierTests.cs | Unit tests for UnresolvedIpClassifier — pinning the reason taxonomy used by the Attack // Statistics tab to group the unresolved-IP sentinel by *why* a logon event lacked a usable // source IP (parser error, missing security-event IP, correlation miss, invalid value, // private/loopback). |

| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/WebTokenVaultTests.cs | Covers the DPAPI machine-scope WebTokenVault: token round-trip, CSPRNG minting (64 hex), atomic tmp+move write, idempotent ACL overlay, refusal on wrong ACL / corrupt or oversized files, DACL gate, and re-hardening when the ACL is permissive. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/WebUiAppSettingsEditorTests.cs | Pins the security-critical appsettings editor used by the Configure IIS workflow: writes ONLY the RdpAudit:Api Enabled/Token surface and the WebUiProxy section, preserves loopback listen address / port / certificate selectors, keeps AllowInlineScripts fail-safe, surfaces pre-edit selectors, and fails curatedly on corrupt or unwritable settings. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/IisWebAccessTests.cs | Pins the DISM state parser (Enabled / Disabled / pending / unknown), the deterministic Install IIS command sets, and the fail-closed IisStatusProbe behaviour including interaction assertions against a stub platform probe. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/WebUiPublisherTests.cs | Pins the WebUiPublisher contract: SHA-256-verified copies, idempotent skips, tampered-file rewrite, secrets-folder refusal, stale removal via manifest, missing-source failure, and the Windows pool ACL grant. |

| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ZabbixSenderProtocolTests.cs | Locks the sender wire format and the accounting rule that a success envelope with failed > 0 is not a delivery. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Core.Tests/ZabbixIncidentPayloadTests.cs | Pins the payload contract: every key present, the maximum severity drives the trigger set, a quiet cycle still pushes the heartbeat, oversize is trimmed into valid JSON, and the host-name normalizer cannot leak an expression character. |

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AbuseIpDbClientTests.cs | HttpMessageHandler-driven tests for the Stage 8 AbuseIpDbClient. The API key never appears in the // test-side logging — only in the simulated HTTP request. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AbuseIpDbReportWorkerTests.cs | End-to-end tests for the Stage 8 AbuseIpDbReportWorker. Confirms it submits reports for // high-threat hostile IPs, respects the local dedup window via AbuseReports, persists // structured results, and never submits when the configuration is disabled. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AutoVacuumModeTests.cs | Pins the auto-vacuum mode of the stores this product creates (B27): a store built through AuditDbInitializer over the interceptor's pragma batch ends in INCREMENTAL and a bounded incremental vacuum really releases pages and shrinks the file, an existing store keeps its mode whichever order the batch uses, the retired WAL-first order leaves a FRESH store in mode NONE (the counterfactual the fix was measured from), the Database-tab overview shows the mode with the freelist and says when online release is impossible, and the maintenance report states the pages it actually released rather than reading the statement's empty result set. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AlertRuleTests.cs | Unit tests covering the alert rule decision boundaries (threshold / whitelist / wrong id). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Alerts/FloodGuardTests.cs | Verifies flood-guard thresholds, isolation, allocation behavior, and critical-evidence bypassing. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Alerts/PipelineLogFloodRuleTests.cs | Verifies delta-based monitoring-pipeline flood alerts, cooldown suppression, and enablement behavior. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ApplicationDataPurgeServiceTests.cs | Tests for the DEBUG-gated full application-data cleanup (Req C). Drives PurgeAllAsync against // an in-memory SQLite database seeded with rows across the accumulated operational tables, and // verifies that those tables are cleared with accurate per-table counts while the preserved // surfaces (DbProps configuration, event-log Bookmarks, schema / migrations) survive — so the // service stays healthy and reachable after a purge. Also covers the already-empty no-op. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Alerts/PrivilegedLoginRuleTests.cs | Pins the hardened PRIVILEGED_LOGIN rule: the well-known service SID filter (S-1-5-18/19/20), the network-context requirement (source IP + LogonType 3/7/10), keyed suppression-window with a single summary alert on expiry, the per-minute alert budget boundary, the historical/backfill age gate, and a zero-allocation assertion over 10 000 rejection-path calls. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AttackStatsAuthAttemptFactDerivationTests.cs | Pins Detect_Attack_Strategy_v3.md §8.1 invariant: Attack Statistics Total / Successful / // Failed counters MUST derive from AuthAttemptFact rows — not from visible RdpConnectionFact // rows, raw RdpCoreTS events, or in-memory connection records. If only RdpCoreTS / TCP // events are present (no AuthAttemptFact), counters remain zero. If a backfill replays the // same Security event that the live watcher already delivered, the fact set must not // double-count. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/BookmarkCheckpointLedgerTests.cs | Contract tests for BookmarkCheckpointLedger - the correlation between a captured bookmark and the ingestion sequence of the event it covers: collect/prune watermark semantics and the ForgetChannel reset path that stops a stale pre-reset bookmark from being re-persisted by the next unified commit. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/CrashGuardBoundedCompletionTests.cs | P2-2 regression test for CrashGuard's fault-handler bound: a hanging OperationLog writer must not stall RecordCrash for the old 3-second wait, while the durable writer is still invoked exactly once with a Critical entry when the database responds in time. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/CrossHostChainAlertProcessorTests.cs | Covers the alert-layer bridge: every unacknowledged CrossHostChain row yields one CROSS_HOST_CHAIN alert candidate per ChainKey with RuleId/severity/per-chain dedup metadata, and acknowledged chains are skipped. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/CrossHostCorrelationWorkerTests.cs | Covers the CrossHostCorrelationWorker background service: 3-host happy-path chain, unavailable-host fail-closed, repeated-poll dedup by DedupKey, disabled skip, cancellation, and SQLITE_BUSY retry. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/CrossHostFactExporterTests.cs | Covers the agent-side CrossHostFactExporter command (ordinal 66): projects AuthAttemptFact rows into bounded CrossHostFactDto summaries with kind/event-id mapping, never emits XML bodies, and fails closed on empty inputs. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/CrossHostIpcTransportTests.cs | Covers the named-pipe cross-host IPC transport: 4-byte LE MessagePack framing, HostName-routed pipe, per-command timeouts, and fail-closed conversion of transport faults. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EditionInfoHandlerTests.cs | Asserts the GetEditionInfo payload against the edition this build was compiled for: ordinal, licence, build constant, every matrix row and the cross-host budget. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventProcessorWorkerCompositionGuardTests.cs | Pins the P2 composition guard on EventProcessorWorker: supplying exactly one of BookmarkStore / BookmarkCheckpointLedger must fail loudly (Critical log line plus a constructor throw) instead of silently degrading to the legacy split-commit behaviour. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AttackStatsIpNormalizationTests.cs | v1.2.1 — pin the IP normalisation contract at the aggregation boundary. The real-host // bug report: a Security 4776 NTLM failure with attempted login "34asdf" surfaced in // Live Events as ".77.37.192.246" (note the leading dot) but Attack Statistics showed // the row under "(unresolved)". Cause: the punctuation-wrapped form failed // IPAddress.TryParse, so the AuthAttemptFact landed with a null IP and the aggregator // bucketed the failure under the unresolved sentinel. // The fix: every layer that writes / reads an IP runs the value through IpNormalizer // first, so ".77.37.192.246", " 77.37.192.246", and "::ffff:77.37.192.246" all collapse // to the same canonical aggregation key "77.37.192.246". |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AttackStatsStaleRebuildV136Tests.cs | Regression coverage for the v1.3.6 stale-RDP-Activity fix. The worker uses a rolling // 30-day look-back anchored on DateTime.UtcNow, so test timestamps are computed relative to // UtcNow rather than hard-coded to avoid rot as calendar time advances. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AuthAttemptFactUpserterTests.cs | v3 invariant coverage (Detect_Attack_Strategy_v3.md §6.3, §8.1, §17). Pins the // atomic-fact contract: every authoritative outcome event creates exactly one // AuthAttemptFact, RdpCoreTS / TCP / WTS never do, NLA-stripped 4625 recovers its IP // from RdpCoreTS 131/140 (−2s … +15s) at High confidence, and a 4625 with LogonType 3 // is still classified as Failed. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/AutoBlockPolicyTests.cs | Unit tests for the Stage 3 auto-block decision function. Covers whitelist skip, // brute-force threshold block, blacklisted-login block, instant-login trip-wire block, // and invalid-IP / missing-IP skip paths. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ChannelHealthPolicyTests.cs | Stage 2 unit tests for ChannelHealthPolicy — covers first-failure bookmark-reset, // cooldown, disable-after-saturation, optional vs critical classification, and the // recovery path after a successful restart. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EnforcementReconciliationServiceTests.cs | Integration-style tests for EnforcementReconciliationService and the reconciliation // worker's row-health mapping, driven against an in-memory SQLite database with a mocked // firewall rule scanner and firewall provider. Covers: a verified block (scanner finds the // rule), an unenforced block (scanner finds nothing -> MissingRule), repair re-installing a // missing rule via the provider, emergency cleanup removing only RdpAudit rules and marking // rows Removed, and the worker demoting an Active row whose enforcement could not be verified. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EtwAvailabilityProbeTests.cs | Verifies the pure ETW availability probe consulted by EventSourceFactorySelector. // Cross-platform: on non-Windows hosts the probe must decline with a reason mentioning // Windows; on Windows we simply assert that the probe returns a well-formed tuple // without throwing. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EtwEventSourceSkeletonTests.cs | v0.2.0 EtwEventSource contract tests. Constructor validation runs on any OS; the // full TraceEventSession lifecycle test is Windows-only and skips silently on other // platforms so the suite stays green on Linux CI. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EtwPayloadXmlFormatterTests.cs | Locks the RDPAudit 2.0 ETW → XML formatter. The formatter's output MUST round-trip // through EventXmlParser and EventNormalizer without loss so downstream alerts, // exports, and UI queries see byte-identical fields regardless of ingestion source. // These tests are pure functions — no ETW, no TraceEvent runtime — so they execute // on Linux CI in the standard test job. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventChannelRingBufferTests.cs | Pins EventChannel's Ring Buffer contract — capacity clamping, power-of-two rounding, // overflow counter initialization and independence across instances. Formerly part of // EventCollectorWorkerRingBufferTests; that class was retired together with the legacy // EventCollectorWorker in iter14, and these facts moved here because they never actually // exercised the worker — only the underlying EventChannel wrapper. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventCollectorHostTests.cs | Contract tests for EventCollectorHost. Uses a hand-rolled fake IEventSourceFactory / // IEventSource pair so the host can be exercised on any platform (net8.0-windows still // links, but no EventLogWatcher is instantiated). Verifies channel lifecycle, bookmark // aggregation with threshold-driven flushing, health-policy dispatch, restart-in-flight // gating, and shutdown correctness. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventCollectorHostedWorkerIntegrationTests.cs | Runtime lifecycle tests for EventCollectorHostedWorker composed against a real // EventCollectorHost with a hand-rolled fake IEventSourceFactory. Verifies: // - Non-Windows ExecuteAsync path idles under Task.Delay and honors cancellation. // - StopAsync flushes pending bookmarks and asks the host to stop all channels. // - Duplicate StopAsync calls are idempotent (no ObjectDisposedException on the // internal linked CancellationTokenSource). // - StopAsync before ExecuteAsync completes cleanly. // - Constructor null-guards fire for every non-optional dependency, and the two // optional dependencies (IDbContextFactory<AuditDbContext>, IOperationLogWriter) // can be omitted without side effects. // - Timing/threshold constants stay pinned (FlushTimerPeriod = 30s, // SecurityBookmarkStalenessThreshold = 15min, SkippedUnavailableReasonMaxLength = 120). // Deliberately does NOT exercise ArmChannelAsync — that path calls // ChannelCapability.Probe which requires a live Windows EventLog subsystem and is // out of scope for a Linux-friendly integration test. EventCollectorHostTests already // pins host arm semantics with the same FakeSource plumbing. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventCollectorHostedWorkerTests.cs | Contract tests for the thin BackgroundService shim EventCollectorHostedWorker. Pins // the XPath-building surface (mirrors EventCollectorWorkerSecurityAuthQueryTests), the // SkippedUnavailable formatter, and the null-guard behavior of the constructor. // Runtime lifecycle tests for the shim itself run on Windows only (they exercise the // host's arm loop, which needs EventLog access) — non-Windows CI still verifies the // static helpers. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventLogWatcherEventSourceTests.cs | Verifies the state machine, callback contract, and idempotency of // EventLogWatcherEventSource without requiring a live Windows Event Log. The tests // exercise the observable transitions (Idle → Running → Stopped, Running → Faulted) // and the guard rails around disposal / double-start / double-stop. // A live Windows-only smoke test lives in a separate integration project so the CI // matrix can skip it on Linux runners. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventNormalizerLogonTypeProcessTests.cs | Stage FIX-2 — locks the per-event LogonType and Process extraction the LiveEvents tab // relies on. Without these tests a regression in EventXmlParser or the normalizer would // silently render LogonType / Process as blanks in the LiveEvents grid. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventNormalizerPositionalFallbackTests.cs | Cameyo-rdpmon-style positional fallback in the normalizer — when a Security 4625 / // 4648 payload omits @Name attributes on its Data children (older Windows builds, audit // policy variants, or stripped event sources), the user / IP must still be recovered // via Properties[5] / Properties[19] / Properties[12]. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventNormalizerStage3SecurityFieldsTests.cs | Stage 3 — pins the contract that the Security 4625 normalizer extracts user, IP, // logon type, status, sub-status, process, port, workstation, auth package and logon // process from the NAMED EventData fields (not Properties[N] index) and canonicalises // NTSTATUS into 0xXXXXXXXX regardless of whether Windows wrote it as signed-decimal, // unsigned-decimal, or hex. Includes the user-reported real-host usernames `md`, the // Cyrillic `ыва`, `sdf`, plus a long username and a SQL-injection-shaped username so // we never regress to a raw-SQL execution path or crash on hostile input. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventNormalizerStage6Tests.cs | Stage 6 normalization tests — Security 4625 with a missing or "-" IpAddress field // must mark RawEvent.SourceIpUnresolved while preserving UserName, and SourceIp must // remain null (no synthetic placeholder). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventNormalizerStageIpATests.cs | End-to-end checks that EventNormalizer attaches direct IP for events that carry one, // attaches a derived IP via SessionCorrelationCache for events that do not, and never // allows a hostname (e.g. 4776 Workstation) to land in RawEvent.SourceIp. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventNormalizerStageIpDTests.cs | Stage IP-D coverage for the TS-RCM 1149 normalization fix. Verifies that Param1 // (UserName), Param2 (Domain) and Param3 (SourceIp) round-trip cleanly into a RawEvent // even though those events use the UserData/EventXML XML schema, and that a 1149-only // observation populates SessionCorrelationCache + drives RdpConnectionFact creation // via RdpConnectionFactUpserter. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventProcessorWorkerRingBufferTests.cs | Validates EventProcessorWorker integration with the Lock-Free SPSC Ring Buffer. // v2.1.0 (iter17): worker now consumes via IEventPipe (RingBufferEventPipe over the // same EventChannel-backed ring). The test writes through channel.Channel.TryWrite // as before — the pipe is a thin adapter and reads out of the exact same physical // ring, so the reflection-invoked DrainBatchAsync still observes every enqueued DTO. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventProcessorWorkerRetainedBatchTests.cs | Locks the retention rule for a batch whose persistence failed: the batch stays reachable for a head-of-line retry and a repeated failure neither replaces it nor restarts its stall clock. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventProcessorWorkerShardCommitTests.cs | Stage 1 regression for the forensic shard commit defect (EventID 7013). After the // main RawEvents transaction commits, shard rows must be written on a dedicated live // SqliteConnection - never by calling BeginTransaction on the EF-owned connection that // EF Core closes together with the disposed transaction. A shard-phase failure must not // rethrow into PersistBatchAsync (which would attempt to roll back the already-committed // RawEvents and discard the pending shard queue); the queue is retained for the next flush. // Adds deterministic seam writers (FailFirstCommitShardWriter / CancelOnCommitShardWriter) // that inject failures INSIDE ShardIngestionSink.CommitAsync via the internal IShardFileWriter // seam: shard commit failure retains RawEvents and the pending queue and recovers on the next flush. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventProcessorWorkerStage6Tests.cs | Stage 6 unit tests for EventProcessorWorker helpers — the Address.UserNames // append helper used to populate per-IP attempted-user history. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/EventSourceFactorySelectorTests.cs | Exercises the pure IngestionMode -> transport selection matrix used by Program.cs // when it registers IEventSourceFactory. Covers the three declared IngestionMode // values (EventLog / Etw / Auto), the out-of-range defensive branch, and both probe // outcomes for the Etw and Auto branches. Runs on any OS (no ETW native calls). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Events/RawEventDtoTests.cs | Verifies default-safe event DTO fields and serializer sequence stamping. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ExternalRdpLoginRuleMessageTests.cs | v1.2.0 fix. The alert message produced by ExternalRdpLoginRule must never end in a // trailing blank "as " when the user name is missing — operators were seeing literal // messages like "External RDP login from public IP 1.2.3.4 as " in the Service tab. // Pin the safe message shape here so a future regression is caught at build time. |

| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/HybridEventSourceFactoryTests.cs | Contract tests for HybridEventSourceFactory. Cross-platform: only inspects // the returned IEventSource type, never calls StartAsync (which is Windows-only). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcDispatcherClearDataTests.cs | Tests for the v1.3.2 ClearAllApplicationData IPC command guard. | tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcDispatcherDedupeTests.cs | Tests for the v1.3.1 DedupeBlocklistEntries IPC command. | tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcDispatcherEventCollectionTests.cs | Verifies catalog-backed Event Collection IPC reads, writes, presets, idempotency, and forever retention. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcDispatcherPaginationTests.cs | Pins deterministic IPC pagination: pages 0/1/2 of a bounded ordered query are non-overlapping, their union equals the full set, and the global order is monotonic across page boundaries. |

| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcDispatcherStage2Tests.cs | Stage 2 visibility coverage — unresolved-IP sentinel rows in Attack Statistics, classifier- // based window summary counters (TS-RCM 1149 / TS-LSM 21 successes counted, 4625 failures // counted), and per-IP historical aggregation feeding the Remote RDP Clients tab. |

| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcDispatcherStripQuotesTests.cs | Pins the defensive address sanitiser used by the Remove-from-blocklist IPC handler. A grid // binding that double-serialized the selected address could arrive wrapped in single or // double quotes (e.g. "\"80.244.40.164\""); StripSurroundingQuotes must peel exactly one // matched outer pair and trim, so the subsequent IP normalization/equality lookup matches the // stored row instead of silently finding nothing. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcLogStabilizationTests.cs | Covers the v1.3.4 IPC log-stabilization behaviour: the accept-loop classifier that // distinguishes an expected client disconnect (logged Debug, never durable) from a genuine // fault (Error + durable Critical), and the operation-log duplicate-collapsing used by the // Logs tab default view so a repeated identical row does not flood the operator's view. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcPipeNameIsolationTests.cs | B4 guard for named-pipe test isolation: pins the production pipe name (still exactly "RdpAuditService", even with plausible override environment variables set in the process), proves a worker constructed the way the composition root constructs it always serves that name and that the internal seam only affects the single instance it is set on, and proves TestPipeNames.Unique() is unique per call, PID-tagged and never the production name. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcServerWorkerInstanceCapTests.cs | Stage 2 regression for the named-pipe instance leak and off-by-one cap. Runs the real // IpcServerWorker against REAL Windows named-pipe clients on a PER-TEST pipe name // (RdpAuditService-<pid>-<guid>, never the production name - see TestPipeNames) and // verifies the acceptance criteria: 12 sequential and 12 parallel Ping clients all succeed // with zero ERROR_PIPE_BUSY (231), a connected-but-silent client is reclaimed by the // per-connection deadline, the live-instance gauge returns to 1 (listener only) after the // traffic drains, and the failure classifier keeps 231 in the "instance cap reached" // bucket instead of the AV/EDR hint bucket. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/IpcServerWorkerLifecycleTests.cs | Regression tests for IpcServerWorker lifecycle and composition-root registration. Pins one ExecuteAsync entry/exit per BackgroundService instance, one stopping-token exit with a non-empty reason, exactly one TimedHostedService wrapping IpcServerWorker in Program.RegisterServices, 12 sequential / 12 parallel real named-pipe clients on a per-test pipe name with zero ERROR_PIPE_BUSY, and silent-client reclamation by the per-connection deadline. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/TestPipeNames.cs | Allocates the per-run pipe name used by the real-pipe integration tests: RdpAuditService-<pid>-<guid>, derived from the production name, unique per test instance, so no other test project - and no product client, which resolves the production name - can ever attach to a pipe a test started. |

| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/MaintenanceWorkerStageIpBRetentionTests.cs | Stage IP-B retention test — verifies MaintenanceWorker prunes stale SessionIpCorrelations // beyond the configured retention window while keeping recent rows. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/MaintenanceWorkerStageIpCRetentionTests.cs | Stage IP-C retention test — verifies MaintenanceWorker prunes stale RdpConnectionFacts // beyond the configured retention window while keeping recent rows. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/MikroTikClientTests.cs | HttpMessageHandler-driven tests for the Stage 9 MikroTikClient. | tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/MpmcEventChannelTests.cs | Contract tests for MpmcEventChannel (the RawEventDto-aware Vyukov ring adapter). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/NetshCommandBuilderTests.cs | Unit tests for the netsh command builder: rule-name normalisation, IP validation, // reserved-address policy, and the argument vectors emitted for add / delete / show // rule actions. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RouteBlackholeProviderTests.cs | Locks the experimental route-blackhole provider's safety contracts: the ping probe fails safe on an ambiguous status (a live-but-ICMP-filtered gateway is refused), and route deletion distinguishes "element not found" (exit 1) from a real failure. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/NetshFirewallRuleScannerTests.cs | Locks the fail-closed contract of the netsh text scanner: a dump the pure parser cannot anchor on (localised labels, changed format, empty output) must be reported as UNSCANNABLE, never as "zero rules" — a zero-rule answer demoted every intended block to MissingRule in reconciliation and kept live OS rules installed forever. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/NetshRunnerRoutingTests.cs | Stage-3 regression suite for the production NetshRunner. Verifies that the parse- // dependent "show allprofiles state" probe is routed through the centralized English // console runner (so the "ON"/"OFF" tokens are emitted in stable Latin script), while // every other invocation — including mutating add-rule / delete-rule — uses direct // argument-list execution (no shell wrapping). Uses a fake IExternalCommandRunner so // the test stays fully host-independent. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/PowerShellFirewallScannerLatchTests.cs | Locks the v2.1.0 PowerShell live-firewall scan latch contract: the first failed or // timed-out PowerShell probe latches the scanner onto the netsh text fallback, every scan // while latched is served WITHOUT spawning PowerShell, PowerShell is re-probed at most once // per the configured retry interval, a successful re-probe releases the latch, and the // current backend plus the latch switch count are published through the injected metrics // sink (the IPC GetStatus surface). The pure latch state machine is exercised directly. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RawEventSerializerTests.cs | Source module (no Purpose header). |

| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RdpConnectionFactUpserterTests.cs | Verifies RdpConnectionFactUpserter merge semantics — creates facts from authoritative // events, updates lifecycle timestamps and counters, rejects hostnames, refuses to create // facts from derived-IP observations, increments FailedLogons / SuccessfulLogons by the // configured event-id taxonomy, and avoids producing duplicates for the same key per batch. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RemoteHostFactSourceTests.cs | Covers RemoteHostFactSource: converts transport / envelope / framing failures into IpcResultStatus.Unavailable DTOs (fail-closed), never invents facts. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RetentionWorkerDecimationTests.cs | Worker-level tests for the retention decimation pass: runs the engine when Storage.Decimation is enabled, skips it when disabled, and keeps prudent counts across the combined prune+decimate tick. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/InformationDensityRetentionWorkerTests.cs | Worker integration tests over real SQLite: anchor byte-identity after overfill, interior-only deletion with a surviving tail, counter non-regression, exact ledger accounting, rare and protected codes untouched, trigger gating (no writes when nothing fires), repeated-pass stability, legal hold, and cancellation mid-pass driven DETERMINISTICALLY from the engine's internal AfterBatchCommitted hook (cancel as soon as one batch is durable) with state assertions - anchor and tail intact, ledger accounting exactly the removed rows, no duplicated ledger window - instead of a deleted-row count or a race against the pass. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RingBufferEventPipeSemaphoreTests.cs | Pins the semaphore-backed WaitToReadAsync contract introduced in RingBufferEventPipe // v2.1.0. Two invariants worth their own file so a regression to polling is caught // immediately: (1) a producer's TryWrite unblocks a concurrent waiter with latency far // below the old 5ms poll interval; (2) repeated writes past a still-pending signal do // NOT throw or leak — the level-triggered signal collapses extra edges. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RingBufferEventPipeTests.cs | Verifies RingBufferEventPipe honours the IEventPipe contract on top of the shipped // RingBufferEventChannel. Pins the invariants Producers/Consumers rely on: // write-then-read round-trip, prefetch/consume ordering under WaitToReadAsync, timeout // semantics, cancellation-safe wait, and forwarded OverflowCount metrics. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RuntimeVersionResolverPinTests.cs | Locks the Service runtime version surfaced via IPC ServiceStatus.Version at exactly // 2.0.2 — the SemVer publish.ps1 emits and the value the Configurator's Service tab // contrasts against the installed and distribution binaries. The complementary core // gate lives in RdpAuditVersionMetadataTests; this one targets the Service assembly // and the resolver path the running service actually uses at runtime. // If the release stream advances, this test must move with it. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/RuntimeVersionResolverTests.cs | Regression coverage for the single-file-safe RuntimeVersionResolver helper. Locks in // the precedence order (AssemblyInformationalVersion > FileVersionInfo from // Environment.ProcessPath > AssemblyName.Version) and verifies the resolver never // throws when the process path is unset, missing, or invalid — the conditions under // which the legacy Assembly.Location-based code would have failed IL3000 analysis or // returned an empty path on a single-file publish. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ElevationTestGuard.cs | The elevation precondition for the real-pipe tests (B21): a cached, FAIL-CLOSED probe of the process token (any probe failure reads as "not elevated") plus the [ElevationFact]/[ElevationTheory] attributes that skip with a recorded reason instead of failing with a misleading UnauthorizedAccessException, because the pipe ACL is the production, Administrators-only one and is deliberately not relaxed for tests. `ResolveSkipReason` is a pure function and the probe is replaceable through an internal seam, so both branches are covered deterministically on an elevated host, where a non-elevated run is unavailable. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ElevationPreconditionMetaTests.cs | Keeps the elevation guard from disappearing again (B21): it registers both branches of the guard through the seam (run, skip, and fail-closed on a throwing probe) and scans the test sources so a test method that drives a real IpcServerWorker without the precondition fails the suite; a recorded per-file count makes the detector itself fail when it stops matching, instead of passing vacuously. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ServiceHostSmokeTests.cs | The end-to-end smoke test (B9): a REAL host built from Program.RegisterServices against a temporary ProgramData root, with the start-up workers running (DatabaseInitializationWorker applies the migrations to a clean store, StoreIntegrityWorker captures the shutdown fingerprint through the real DPAPI-protected chain) and the Database tab's whole command set driven through the REAL dispatcher - overview, retention policy, dry-run preview, snapshot create/list/verify, ledger, and a subject-purge DRY RUN. It records the 20 hosted services it deliberately does not start (the production pipe name, the Kestrel listener, the event-log subscriptions, the firewall/netsh workers and the background loops) and asserts that count, so a newly added worker forces the decision. This test is what found the closed-connection defect in the overview's Ledger section. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SingleInstanceGuardTests.cs | Windows-only unit tests for SingleInstanceGuard (SingleInstanceWindowsFact skips on non-Windows hosts). Each test uses a unique Global\RdpAudit.Service.Tests.<guid> mutex and `using`-scoped guards so the cases are deterministic and never leak handles across tests. Pins: TryAcquire returns true on first acquire, false on a second concurrent acquire from the same process; ownership becomes available again after Dispose; an AbandonedMutexException is recovered as RecoveredAbandoned without throwing and the recovered guard keeps ownership; the DACL-creation refusal falls back to the process-default DACL; GetExitCode returns 0 for Acquired / Recovered states and 0x1000 for AlreadyRunning, distinct from the Win32-looking 0x2 and the host-fault 1; and TryAcquire is idempotent on the same guard instance. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Security4625RealHostIngestionTests.cs | v1.2.0 real-host integration test. The user-reported brute-force evidence on a real // Windows host showed Security 4625 with users `md`, `ыва`, `sdf`, IP 77.37.192.246, // LogonType 3, signed-decimal Status -1073741715, SubStatus -1073741718 / -1073741724. // RawEvents=393 AuthAttemptFacts=0 was the symptom. This test replays exactly that // shape end-to-end (Normalize → AuthAttemptFactUpserter → AttackStatsRefreshWorker) and // pins the outcome: failed AuthAttemptFact rows are created immediately, with IP, user, // LogonType, canonicalised NTSTATUS, and Attack Statistics shows Failed > 0 with // First/Last fact populated for 77.37.192.246. |

| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Scenarios/ShardCommitDurabilityScenarios.cs | Tier-3 end-to-end scenario coverage for the shard durability contract, driven through EventProcessorWorker.ExecuteAsync and a real in-process IEventPipe: happy path (RawEvents commit, truthful shard metadata, empty pending queue), shard-commit failure inside the sink (RawEvents durable, queue retained, next flush recovers), and shard-phase cancellation (committed RawEvents survive, queue untouched). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityAuthProbeServiceTests.cs | v1.2.0 probe contract. On non-Windows hosts (Linux CI / dev) the probe must report // Outcome=NotWindows with IpcResultStatus.Unavailable rather than throwing — that is // the canonical signal a Configurator running against a non-service host receives. // The XPath emitted is also verified so a regression that re-widens the probe query // fails immediately, plus the exception-classifier path used by the long-running // collector to disambiguate AccessDenied / ChannelNotFound / Error. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityBackfillPerIdMetricsTests.cs | v1.2.2 — pin the per-id Security backfill diagnostic surface on ServiceMetrics: // per-id snapshots are recorded and recallable, the clear-stale helper drops every // Security::Backfill::<id> channel-status entry and every per-id snapshot in one // hop, and per-id NoEvents must never set LastSecurityChannelError (a workstation // without DC events must NOT paint the top-level Security channel as broken). |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityBackfillWorkerIsNoEventsTests.cs | Regression coverage for SecurityBackfillWorker.IsNoEventsMessage classification and // for the structural shape of the per-EventID XPath used by the backfill worker. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityBackfillWorkerNonFatalTests.cs | v1.2.1 stabilisation — pin the non-fatal per-id classification contract for // SecurityBackfillWorker. A 1102 audit-log-cleared query that times out on a real host // while 4624/4625/4776 keep flowing must NOT paint the Security channel as broken at // the top level. The previous behaviour set "Last Security channel error" for every // per-id QueryFailed, scaring operators into thinking ingestion had stalled when in // fact 4624/4625/4776 were still being forwarded. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityBackfillWorkerTests.cs | Pure-helper tests for the Security backfill worker. The EventLogReader poll path is // Windows-only and exercised by manual QA on a real host; here we lock the XPath shape // and the in-memory dedup ring contract that bound the worker's CPU/memory footprint. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityBackfillWorkerV12ContractTests.cs | v1.2.0 invariants for the Security backfill path. The previous implementation issued // one giant OR-clause XPath covering the full v3 set and timed out on hosts with large // Security logs, leaving every poll in QueryFailed. The new path splits per-EventID // with ReverseDirection=true and a hard MaxRowsPerEventId ceiling so a per-id timeout // never starves the other ids. These tests pin the constants the runtime relies on and // the per-id XPath shape; the actual EventLogReader poll path is Windows-only and // exercised by manual QA on a real host. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityCorrelationWatchdogTests.cs | Locks the diagnostic the watchdog raises when TS-RCM 261 / RdpCoreTS 131 fire without // a matching Security 4624/4625/4648 inside the correlation window — the operator- // visible symptom of disabled audit policy or missing Security log read privilege. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SecurityVisibilityDiagnosticBuilderTests.cs | Pins the derivation rules for the discrete Security visibility diagnostic flags // surfaced via IPC ServiceStatus (SecurityLogMissing, AuditPolicyMissingLogon, // SecurityReadDenied, ChannelDisabled, BookmarkStaleOrLogRetentionGap). Each test // arranges a single failure mode and asserts only the matching flag flips so the UI // chip lights for the right reason. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SessionCorrelationCacheHydrationTests.cs | Verifies the Stage IP-B hydration path — SessionCorrelationCache populates its // three indexes from persisted SessionIpCorrelations and resolves lookups by LogonId, // (SessionId, UserName), and UserName after a single hydration pass. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SessionCorrelationCacheTests.cs | Verifies SessionCorrelationCache resolves IPs via LogonId, (SessionId, UserName), and // UserName indexes, that TTL expiry skips stale entries, and that capacity-bounded // eviction trims the oldest observations first. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SessionIpCorrelationUpserterTests.cs | Verifies SessionIpCorrelationUpserter merge semantics — first observation creates a // row, subsequent observations under the same key refresh LastSeenUtc / ObservedEventIds // without producing duplicates, hostnames are rejected, and at most one logical upsert // per unique correlation key occurs per batch. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/SettingsManagerSecretsTests.cs | Tests that SettingsManager wraps secret fields (AbuseIpDb.ApiKey, MikroTik.Password) with // ISecretProtector before persistence. The plaintext value must never appear in the saved // appsettings.json document. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Storage/IpEventSummaryUpserterShardMetadataTests.cs | Pins the rule that IpEventSummaryUpserter never fabricates shard metadata, and that the aggregate counters it does own stay correct. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/Storage/ShardIngestionSinkTests.cs | Verifies optional shard ingestion writes only truthful on-disk metadata. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/TestAuthAttemptFactHelper.cs | Shared seeding helper for tests that need to round-trip the v3 atomic fact contract — // for every Security 4624 / 4625 / 4648 RawEvent inserted by a test setup, also create // the AuthAttemptFact row the production pipeline would have written. Centralised here so // a future refactor of the upserter does not require touching every test fixture. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ThirdPartyFirewallClassifierTests.cs | Regression suite for FirewallProviderClassifier — proves that the // exact real-world signal set observed in the user's diagnostic bundle // (klim6 running, avp.exe / avp.com present, SecurityCenter2 = Kaspersky) // yields ThirdPartyFirewallSuspected = true, and profiles per-call heap // allocation across every classification branch so a hot-path regression // shows up as a failing test with the exact byte count, not a silent // performance drift. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/UtcTimestampEnricherTests.cs | Pins the Serilog enricher behind the DEBUG text mirror's UTC 'Z'-suffixed timestamps: the UtcTimestamp property is added with offset zero and the same instant, the original event timestamp is never mutated, and already-UTC events still get the property. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ToolsDiagnosticsServiceTests.cs | Exercises ToolsDiagnosticsService with fake runner / firewall provider implementations so // the read-only probe set and the temporary-firewall-rule create / verify / cleanup // orchestration are testable on Linux CI without spawning a Windows command. Pins the // stdout-on-empty-stderr capture, the per-step backend command detail, and the overall // create+verify+cleanup verdict. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/UnmanagedMpmcRingBufferTests.cs | Behavioural and stress tests for the Vyukov MPMC ring buffer that backs // v2.0 multi-producer event ingestion. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/UnmanagedSpscRingBufferTests.cs | Validates the low-level UnmanagedSpscRingBuffer strict FIFO semantics, // memory safety, cache-line padding, and concurrent monotonic ordering. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/WindowsFirewallProviderTests.cs | Unit tests for the WindowsFirewallProvider. Verifies that the provider validates // inputs, refuses reserved addresses by policy, drives netsh idempotently via the // ArgumentList path, and surfaces controlled DTOs instead of raw exceptions. |

| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ZabbixSenderClientTests.cs | Drives the TCP client against a real loopback listener, so the frame layout, the answer prefix and the rejection accounting are proven end to end. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ZabbixApiRequestBodyTests.cs | Pins the JSON-RPC request shape: `params` is always present, because a live Zabbix 8.0.0 rejects `apiinfo.version` without it. |
| tests/RdpAudit.Service.Tests/ZabbixPushStateStoreTests.cs | Proves the crash-consistency contract of the push position: round-trip, no temporary file left behind, corrupt file preserved aside. |

### RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks.Tests

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| tests/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks.Tests/StorageBenchmarkRunnerSmokeTests.cs | Runs the storage benchmark end to end, in process, against a small temporary store on the --quick profile. Checks the three artifacts and the sections an operator reads, the documented exit codes, both refusal paths, and - the reason the test exists - that %ProgramData%/RdpAudit is bit-for-bit unchanged afterwards, compared by a digest over every path, length and last-write time in the tree. |

### RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/BenchmarkSampleRecorder.cs | The one place that decides what a sample means: warm-up runs, then timed runs reduced with the same arithmetic for every metric. A probe that throws is recorded as failed with its exception text, never as a zero. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/BenchmarkStore.cs | One benchmark store and the product options that point at it, plus the repetition model: a retention sample runs against its own copy of the seeded store so every sample starts from byte-identical state. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/BenchmarkWorkspace.cs | The safety boundary of the tool. Owns every directory a run creates; refuses a --store that already holds rdpaudit.db unless the operator overrides it (and then works on a copy); refuses any store or report path inside the production ProgramData RdpAudit tree, with no override. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/BackupProbe.cs | Snapshots, snapshot verification and the shard-inclusive snapshot, driven through the real DatabaseBackupService. Writes a real shard tree with the product's own ShardWriter and ShardPath so the bytes being copied are production-shaped. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/DirectDbContextFactory.cs | The smallest IDbContextFactory a product service that consumes one needs, built with the same options construction the service host uses, so the benchmark store carries production's WAL and cache pragmas. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/IntegrityProbe.cs | quick_check, full integrity_check and foreign_key_check durations, running the same PRAGMA statements the maintenance service emits - but on a read-only connection, because that service reports TimedOut instead of a duration once a step passes its 30-second limit. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/LedgerProbe.cs | Chained deletion-ledger appends through the real writer, one transaction per entry - the granularity the retention batch actually uses, since the ledger append sits inside the writer-lock window. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/MachineProfileProbe.cs | Collects the machine profile and measures the store volume, including flushed small-write latency and the media verdict: the hardware query where the platform answers, otherwise an explicitly labelled inference from that measurement. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/Program.cs | Process entry point: hands the command line to the runner, prints a refusal, turns an unexpected exception into a non-zero exit code. Contains no measurement logic. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/ReclaimProbe.cs | The WAL checkpoint, the bounded incremental vacuum and the offline compaction, each measured against a freshly prepared dirty store because all three are one-shot operations. Drives the real DatabaseCompactService with a service name that cannot exist. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/RetentionProbe.cs | The information-density retention pass and its dry run, through the real InformationDensityRetentionEngine. The writer-lock hold comes from the engine's own per-batch observation hook. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/SeedProbe.cs | Fills a fresh store with synthetic raw events through the product's own EF migrations and times the fill, per 100 000-row chunk. The bulk load is reported as a bulk load, not as the service's ingestion path. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/SimpleOptionsMonitor.cs | A fixed IOptionsMonitor over one immutable value, so the real product services can be driven with a configuration the run constructed deliberately instead of standing up a host. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/SqliteBenchmarkFacts.cs | Raw SQLite reads and timing helpers shared by the probes, including freelist accounting kept separate from file length and the pool clearing that lets a probe delete the copy it just measured. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/StorageBenchmarkCli.cs | Command-line parsing and help text, hand-rolled so the tool depends on nothing an operator's machine may not have. Every rejection names the option and the accepted range. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/StorageBenchmarkExitCode.cs | The exit codes, in one place, so a wrapper script can tell "the command line was wrong" from "the run refused to start" from "a probe failed". |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/StorageBenchmarkOptions.cs | The validated shape of one invocation, with the bounds of the run stated in one place and the reason for each bound. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/StorageBenchmarkRunner.cs | Runs the probes in a fixed order and assembles the report. Also where the honesty rules are enforced: a failed probe contributes a failed metric and a distinct exit code, a probe that was not reached contributes a not-measured metric with its reason, and no path substitutes a default for a measurement. |
| tools/RdpAudit.StorageBenchmarks/WindowsFacts.cs | The platform facts a machine profile needs, collected without administrator rights and independently guarded: a host where an API is unavailable or a registry key is missing yields "unknown" for that one fact. |
